### [31] [ECM Contracts: Contract-Aware, Versioned, and Governable Capability Interfaces for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13097)
*Xue Qin,Simin Luan,John See,Cong Yang,Zhijun Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出ECM Contracts模型，为具身能力模块提供六维契约接口，支持静态检查和版本兼容性管理，构建稳定的具身软件生态系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身能力模块(ECMs)缺乏系统化的组合和发布机制，无法形成稳定的软件生态系统，需要超越传统软件接口的契约模型来支持具身执行的特殊需求。

Method: 提出ECM Contracts接口模型，包含功能签名、行为假设、资源需求、权限边界、恢复语义和版本兼容性六个维度；建立兼容性框架进行静态和部署前检查；设计具身能力的发布规范，包括版本感知兼容性类别、弃用规则、迁移约束和政策敏感升级检查。

Result: 原型系统评估显示，契约感知的组合显著减少了不安全或无效的模块组合；契约指导的发布检查相比仅基于模式或临时基线的方法，提升了升级安全性和回滚准备度。

Conclusion: 稳定的具身软件生态系统不仅需要模块化封装，更需要显式契约来连接能力组合、治理和演化，ECM Contracts为此提供了系统化解决方案。

Abstract: Embodied agents increasingly rely on modular capabilities that can be installed, upgraded, composed, and governed at runtime. Prior work has introduced embodied capability modules (ECMs) as reusable units of embodied functionality, and recent research has explored their runtime governance and controlled evolution. However, a key systems question remains unresolved: how can ECMs be composed and released as a stable software ecosystem rather than as ad hoc skill bundles?
  We present ECM Contracts, a contract-based interface model for embodied capability modules. Unlike conventional software interfaces that specify only input and output types, ECM Contracts encode six dimensions essential for embodied execution: functional signature, behavioral assumptions, resource requirements, permission boundaries, recovery semantics, and version compatibility. Based on this model, we introduce a compatibility framework for ECM installation, composition, and upgrade, enabling static and pre-deployment checks for type mismatches, dependency conflicts, policy violations, resource contention, and recovery incompatibilities.
  We further propose a release discipline for embodied capabilities, including version-aware compatibility classes, deprecation rules, migration constraints, and policy-sensitive upgrade checks. We implement a prototype ECM registry, resolver, and contract checker, and evaluate the approach on modular embodied tasks in a robotics runtime setting. Results show that contract-aware composition substantially reduces unsafe or invalid module combinations, and that contract-guided release checks improve upgrade safety and rollback readiness compared with schema-only or ad hoc baselines.
  Our findings suggest that stable embodied software ecosystems require more than modular packaging: they require explicit contracts that connect capability composition, governance, and evolution.

</details>


### [32] [Fairness in Multi-Agent Systems for Software Engineering: An SDLC-Oriented Rapid Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13103)
*Corey Yang-Smith,Ronnie de Souza Santos,Ahmad Abdellatif*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文对多智能体系统（MAS）中的公平性研究进行了快速综述，特别关注LLM赋能环境及其在软件工程中的相关性，识别了当前研究的三个主要差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于Transformer的大型语言模型（LLMs）和多智能体系统（MAS）在软件开发生命周期（SDLC）中的广泛应用，这些开发者工具中的公平性影响尚未得到充分探索，尽管它们在塑造代码编写、审查和发布过程中发挥着日益重要的作用。

Method: 采用快速综述方法，从初始的350篇论文中筛选和过滤，最终保留18项研究进行分析。分析框架包括公平性的定义、评估方法和报告的危害，并将其与SDLC阶段关联。

Result: 研究发现公平性被定义为可信AI原则、群体间偏见减少和集体互动动态的组合；评估方法包括偏见基准的准确性指标、人口统计差异度量以及MAS特有的概念（如从众和偏见放大）。报告的危害包括代表性、服务质量、安全隐私和治理失败。识别了三个持续性差距：碎片化且缺乏MAS特定性的评估实践、因简化环境和狭窄属性覆盖导致的有限泛化能力、以及与真实软件工作流对齐的缓解和治理机制稀缺且评估薄弱。

Conclusion: 当前MAS公平性研究尚未准备好支持可部署的、公平性有保障的软件系统，需要开发MAS感知的基准测试、一致的协议以及贯穿整个生命周期的治理机制。

Abstract: Transformer-based large language models (LLMs) and multi-agent systems (MAS) are increasingly embedded across the software development lifecycle (SDLC), yet their fairness implications for developer-facing tools remain underexplored despite their growing role in shaping what code is written, reviewed, and released. We present a rapid review of recent work on fairness in MAS, emphasizing LLM-enabled settings and relevance to software engineering. Starting from an initial set of 350 papers, we screened and filtered the corpus for relevance, retaining 18 studies for final analysis. Across these 18 studies, fairness is framed as a combination of trustworthy AI principles, bias reduction across groups, and interactional dynamics in collectives, while evaluation spans accuracy metrics on bias benchmarks, demographic disparity measures, and emergent MAS-specific notions such as conformity and bias amplification. Reported harms include representational, quality-of-service, security and privacy, and governance failures, which we relate to SDLC stages where evidence is most and least developed. We identify three persistent gaps: (1) fragmented, rarely MAS-specific evaluation practices that limit comparability, (2) limited generalization due to simplified environments and narrow attribute coverage, and (3) scarce, weakly evaluated mitigation and governance mechanisms aligned to real software workflows. These findings suggest MAS fairness research is not yet ready to support deployable, fairness-assured software systems, motivating MAS-aware benchmarks, consistent protocols, and lifecycle-spanning governance.

</details>


### [33] [Building Trust in the Skies: A Knowledge-Grounded LLM-based Framework for Aviation Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13101)
*Anirudh Iyengar,Alisa Tiselska,Dumindu Samaraweera,Hong Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出结合大语言模型和知识图谱的端到端框架，提升航空安全分析的可靠性和可验证性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在航空安全决策中存在事实错误、幻觉和不可验证等固有局限，这些缺陷在安全关键环境中可能导致灾难性后果，因此需要提高LLM在安全分析中的可信度

Method: 提出双阶段管道：首先使用LLM从多模态数据自动构建和动态更新航空安全知识图谱，然后在检索增强生成架构中利用该知识图谱来锚定、验证和解释LLM生成的响应

Result: 实现系统相比纯LLM方法展现出更高的准确性和可追溯性，有效支持复杂查询并减轻幻觉问题，能够提供上下文感知、可验证的安全洞察

Conclusion: 该框架成功解决了航空行业对可靠性的严格要求，未来工作将聚焦于增强关系提取和集成混合检索机制

Abstract: The integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) into aviation safety decision-making represents a significant technological advancement, yet their standalone application poses critical risks due to inherent limitations such as factual inaccuracies, hallucination, and lack of verifiability. These challenges undermine the reliability required for safety-critical environments where errors can have catastrophic consequences. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a novel, end-to-end framework that synergistically combines LLMs and Knowledge Graphs (KGs) to enhance the trustworthiness of safety analytics. The framework introduces a dual-phase pipeline: it first employs LLMs to automate the construction and dynamic updating of an Aviation Safety Knowledge Graph (ASKG) from multimodal sources. It then leverages this curated KG within a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) architecture to ground, validate, and explain LLM-generated responses. The implemented system demonstrates improved accuracy and traceability over LLM-only approaches, effectively supporting complex querying and mitigating hallucination. Results confirm the framework's capability to deliver context-aware, verifiable safety insights, addressing the stringent reliability requirements of the aviation industry. Future work will focus on enhancing relationship extraction and integrating hybrid retrieval mechanisms.

</details>


### [34] [Zero-shot Evaluation of Deep Learning for Java Code Clone Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13783)
*Thomas S. Heinze*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DL在克隆检测中应用广泛，但在零样本评估场景下，基于DL的克隆检测器对未见代码的泛化能力有限，传统工具NiCad甚至表现更优


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究基于深度学习的Java代码克隆检测器的泛化能力，特别是在零样本评估场景下，探索DL模型对未见代码的适应性和性能表现

Method: 复制并评估五个最先进的DL克隆检测器（包括CodeBERT等Transformer模型和FA-AST+GMN等单任务模型），在零样本评估场景下进行训练/微调和评估，使用不同数据集和功能

Result: 实验表明模型对未见代码的泛化能力有限，进一步分析发现传统克隆检测器NiCad在零样本评估场景下甚至优于基于DL的克隆检测器

Conclusion: 基于深度学习的克隆检测器在实际零样本场景中的泛化能力存在局限性，传统方法在某些情况下仍具有优势，需要进一步改进DL模型的泛化性能

Abstract: Deep Learning (DL) is becoming more and more widespread in clone detection, motivated by achieving near-perfect performance for this task. In particular in case of semantic code clones, which share only limited syntax but implement the same or similar functionality, Deep Learning appears to outperform conventional tools. In this paper, we want to investigate the generalizability of DL-based clone detectors for Java. We therefore replicate and evaluate the performance of five state-of-the-art DL-based clone detectors, including Transformers like CodeBERT and single-task models like FA-AST+GMN, in a zero-shot evaluation scenario, where we train/fine-tune and evaluate on different datasets and functionalities. Our experiments demonstrate that the models' generalizability to unseen code is limited. Further analysis reveals that the conventional clone detector NiCad even outperforms the DL-based clone detectors in such a zero-shot evaluation scenario.

</details>


### [35] [Applying an Agentic Coding Tool for Improving Published Algorithm Implementations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13109)
*Worasait Suwannik*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一个两阶段AI辅助改进已发表算法实现的流程：第一阶段用大语言模型筛选符合实验标准的近期算法，第二阶段用Claude Code复现基线并迭代改进


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索AI如何辅助改进已发表的算法实现，分析人类在AI辅助研究中的不可替代作用，讨论对同行评审和学术出版的影响

Method: 两阶段流程：1) 使用具备研究能力的大语言模型筛选近期发表的符合明确实验标准的算法；2) 使用Claude Code接收提示来复现报告的基线，然后进行迭代改进过程

Result: 在跨多个研究领域的11个实验中，Claude Code报告所有实验都获得了改进，每个改进可在单个工作日内完成

Conclusion: 人类在目标选择、实验有效性验证、新颖性和影响评估、计算资源提供以及适当的AI使用披露写作等方面仍然不可或缺，这对同行评审和学术出版具有重要意义

Abstract: We present a two-stage pipeline for AI-assisted improvement of published algorithm implementations. In the first stage, a large language model with research capabilities identifies recently published algorithms satisfying explicit experimental criteria. In the second stage, Claude Code is given a prompt to reproduce the reported baseline and then iterate an improvement process. We apply this pipeline to published algorithm implementations spanning multiple research domains. Claude Code reported that all eleven experiments yielded improvements. Each improvement could be achieved within a single working day. We analyse the human contributions that remain indispensable, including selecting the target, verifying experimental validity, assessing novelty and impact, providing computational resources, and writing with appropriate AI-use disclosure. Finally, we discuss implications for peer review and academic publishing.

</details>


### [36] [The Code Whisperer: LLM and Graph-Based AI for Smell and Vulnerability Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13114)
*Mohammad Baqar,Raji Rustamov,Alexander Hughes*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出了Code Whisperer混合框架，结合图分析和LLM检测、解释和修复代码异味与安全漏洞


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码异味和软件漏洞都会增加维护成本，但现有工具通常是分离的，缺乏结构上下文且产生噪声警告

Method: 结合图分析（AST、CFG、PDG）与大语言模型，统一检测、解释和修复代码问题

Result: 混合设计相比纯图分析或纯语言模型方法，提高了检测性能并产生更有用的修复建议

Conclusion: 该框架为日常软件工程工作流中采用AI辅助代码审查提供了实用的解决方案

Abstract: Code smells and software vulnerabilities both increase maintenance cost, yet they are often handled by separate tools that miss structural context and produce noisy warnings. This paper presents The Code Whisperer, a hybrid framework that combines graph-based program analysis with large language models to detect, explain, and repair maintainability and security issues within a unified workflow. The method aligns Abstract Syntax Trees (ASTs), Control Flow Graphs (CFGs), Program Dependency Graphs (PDGs), and token-level code embeddings so that structural and semantic signals can be learned jointly. We evaluate the framework on multi-language datasets and compare it with rule-based analyzers and single-model baselines. The results indicate that the hybrid design improves detection performance and produces more useful repair suggestions than either graph-only or language-model-only approaches. We also examine explainability and CI/CD integration as practical requirements for adopting AI-assisted code review in everyday software engineering workflows.

</details>


### [37] [Modeling and Simulation Based Engineering in the Context of Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13118)
*Alexandre Muzy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MSBE方法将执行语义作为一等工程实体，通过形式化执行条件来弥合CPS中已验证模型行为与已验证执行行为之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CPS工程方法不将执行语义作为一等工程实体处理，形式验证在固定语义假设下进行，仿真验证的执行语义由仿真引擎隐式决定，物理约束被视为实现细节而非语义边界条件。

Method: 提出建模与仿真基础工程(MSBE)方法，将执行条件形式化为四个组件：执行语义、活动、可容许约束和指定属性，围绕迭代循环组织工程过程，交替进行形式执行、实验执行、验证和活动介导的验证。

Result: MSBE框架应用于四类CPS系统（人本、生物物理、技术和数字孪生），证明该框架可推广到任何行为依赖于明确定义执行条件的系统。

Conclusion: 将执行语义作为一等工程实体是弥合CPS中已验证模型行为与已验证执行行为之间差距的必要且充分条件，MSBE为此提供了系统化方法论。

Abstract: Cyber-Physical Systems (CPS) produce behavior through execution on substrates coupling computation with physical processes. However, usual engineering approaches do not treat execution semantics as first-class engineering entities. Formal verification reasons about model behaviors under fixed semantic assumptions that are not revisable and do not account for physical execution constraints. Simulation-based validation explores scenarios under execution semantics that are implicitly determined by the simulation engine. In both cases, physical constraints of the execution substrate are addressed as implementation details rather than as semantic boundary conditions. In this article, it is hypothesized that making execution semantics explicit as first-class engineering entities is necessary and sufficient to bridge the gap between verified model behaviors and validated executed behaviors in CPS. To test this hypothesis, Modeling and Simulation Based Engineering (MSBE) is proposed: a methodology grounded in the Theory of Modeling and Simulation. MSBE formalizes execution conditions as four components: execution semantics, activity (behaviorally meaningful changes), admissibility constraints (physical bounds), and specified properties (behavioral guarantees). MSBE organizes engineering around an iterative cycle alternating formal execution, experimental execution, verification, and activity-mediated validation. Executability is defined as stabilization of execution conditions and the induced admissible model space. The cycle is applied to four CPS classes (human-centric, biophysical, technological, and digital twins). These applications show that the framework generalizes beyond CPS to any system whose behavior depends on explicitly defined execution conditions. Modeling and Simulation-Based Engineering

</details>


### [38] [AgentForge: Execution-Grounded Multi-Agent LLM Framework for Autonomous Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13120)
*Rajesh Kumar,Waqar Ali,Junaid Ahmed,Najma Imtiaz Ali,Shaban Usman*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AGENTFORGE是一个基于执行验证的多智能体框架，通过强制Docker沙箱执行验证代码变更，在SWE-BENCH Lite上达到40.0%的解决率，显著优于单智能体基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型能生成看似合理的代码但无法验证正确性，现有的多智能体系统要么模拟执行要么将验证设为可选。需要将执行验证作为核心原则，确保每个代码变更在传播前都经过沙箱执行验证。

Method: 提出执行验证原则，并实例化为AGENTFORGE框架：包含Planner、Coder、Tester、Debugger和Critic五个智能体，通过共享内存和强制Docker沙箱协调工作。将LLM软件工程形式化为基于仓库状态的迭代决策过程，执行反馈提供比下一个词概率更强的监督信号。

Result: 在SWE-BENCH Lite上达到40.0%的解决率，比单智能体基线高出26-28个百分点。消融实验证实执行反馈和角色分解各自独立驱动性能提升。

Conclusion: 执行验证应作为多智能体代码生成系统的核心原则，AGENTFORGE框架通过强制沙箱执行和角色分解显著提升了代码生成和修复能力，为LLM软件工程提供了更可靠的监督机制。

Abstract: Large language models generate plausible code but cannot verify correctness. Existing multi-agent systems simulate execution or leave verification optional. We introduce execution-grounded verification as a first-class principle: every code change must survive sandboxed execution before propagation. We instantiate this principle in AGENTFORGE, a multi-agent framework where Planner, Coder, Tester, Debugger, and Critic agents coordinate through shared memory and a mandatory Docker sandbox. We formalize software engineering with LLMs as an iterative decision process over repository states, where execution feedback provides a stronger supervision signal than next-token likelihood. AGENTFORGE achieves 40.0\% resolution on SWE-BENCH Lite, outperforming single-agent baselines by 26--28 points. Ablations confirm that execution feedback and role decomposition each independently drive performance. The framework is open-source at https://github.com/raja21068/AutoCodeAI.

</details>


### [39] [How Developers Adopt, Use, and Evolve CI/CD Caching: An Empirical Study on GitHub Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13129)
*Kazi Amit Hasan,Yuan Tian,Safwat Hassan,Steven H. H. Ding*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 首次对GitHub Actions中CI/CD缓存配置与演化的实证研究，分析了952个仓库，发现缓存采用仓库更活跃，缓存配置多样且维护频繁，参数更新多为人工修复问题，版本更新多为机器人维护依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CI/CD缓存虽能减少重复计算、提高效率，但需要持续维护。目前缺乏对开发者如何配置和演化CI/CD缓存的实际研究，需要了解缓存使用模式、配置演化规律及变更驱动因素。

Method: 对GitHub Actions上的CI/CD缓存进行实证研究，分析952个GitHub仓库（266个缓存采用者，686个非采用者），涵盖1,556个工作流文件、10,373次提交和17,185个工作流配置变更。从仓库特征、缓存使用模式、配置演化模式、变更驱动因素四个维度进行分析。

Result: 主要发现：(1) 缓存采用仓库比非采用者更活跃和受欢迎；(2) 缓存通过多种机制在不同CI/CD任务类型中使用，无标准化方法；(3) 缓存配置通过频繁、重复的维护模式演化，构建和测试任务更新快，其他任务演化慢；(4) 缓存相关修改由不同维护需求驱动：参数更新多为人工修复问题，版本更新多为机器人维护依赖。

Conclusion: CI/CD缓存需要大量维护工作，平均每个仓库有9.37次缓存相关变更。研究量化了缓存维护工作量，揭示了改进可靠性和工具支持的机会，为自动化缓存维护和优化提供了实证基础。

Abstract: Continuous Integration/Continuous Delivery (CI/CD) caching is widely used to reduce repeated computation and improve CI/CD efficiency, yet maintaining effective caching requires ongoing maintenance effort. In this paper, we present the first empirical study on how developers configure and evolve caching in CI/CD workflows on GitHub Actions. We analyze 952 GitHub repositories (266 cache adopters and 686 non-adopters), to compare repository characteristics, characterize caching usage at the job and step levels, uncover patterns in caching configuration evolution, and identify the drivers of cache-related changes. Our analysis spans 1,556 workflow files, 10,373 commits, and 17,185 workflow configuration changes, including an average of 9.37 cache-related changes per repository. Our main observations are: (1) cache-adopting repositories are more active and popular than non-adopters; (2) caching is used across multiple CI/CD job types through a variety of caching mechanisms rather than a single standardized approach; (3) caching configurations evolve through frequent, repetitive maintenance patterns, with rapid updates in build and test jobs and slower evolution in other job types; and (4) cache-related modifications are driven by distinct maintenance needs: parameter updates are mainly human-driven to fix issues, while version updates occur later and are often bot-driven for dependency maintenance. Our findings quantify the substantial maintenance effort involved in CI/CD caching and highlight opportunities to improve reliability and tool support.

</details>


### [40] [Analysis of Commit Signing on Github](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14014)
*Abubakar Sadiq Shittu,John Sadik,Farzin Gholamrezae,Scott Ruoti*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: GitHub提交签名采用率看似很高，但平台范围用户中心分析显示实际开发者签名率极低（<6%），大多数签名仅来自Web界面，本地签名不持久且存在密钥管理问题，无法作为可靠的软件供应链来源信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通过单个仓库或精选项目样本研究提交签名，无法全面了解开发者在整个平台上的行为模式。需要从平台范围、用户中心的角度分析提交签名的实际采用情况，以评估其作为软件供应链安全基础的可靠性。

Method: 基于可复制性理论，将采样单位从仓库转变为个体开发者。追踪71,694名活跃GitHub用户（至少提交过一次提交），涵盖他们所有仓库和完整提交历史，共分析1,600万次提交和874,198个仓库。排除平台生成的签名，分析开发者实际签名行为、签名持续性、跨仓库签名模式以及密钥管理问题。

Result: 1. 生态系统表面高签名采用率是假象：排除平台生成签名后，仅不到6%的开发者曾亲自签名提交；2. 大多数表面签名者从未在Web浏览器外签名；3. 少数本地签名者中，签名很少随时间或跨仓库持续；4. 约1/8的开发者管理签名验证失败（密钥未上传至GitHub）；5. 密钥注册表中过期密钥几乎从不撤销，超过1/4用户至少有一个失效密钥。

Conclusion: 当前实践的提交签名无法作为生态系统规模上可靠的来源信号。需要改进签名工具、简化密钥管理、增强开发者教育，并提供具体建议来弥补这一差距，使提交签名真正成为软件供应链安全的有效基础。

Abstract: Commit signing is widely promoted as a foundation of software supply-chain security, yet prior work has studied it through the lens of individual repositories or curated project samples, missing the broader picture of how developers behave across an entire platform. Grounded in replicability theory, we vary the sampling unit from repositories to individual developers, following 71,694 active GitHub users, defined as accounts that have authored at least one commit, across all their repositories and their entire commit history, spanning 16 million commits and 874,198 repositories. This platform-wide, user-centric view reveals a fundamental gap that repository sampling cannot detect. The ecosystem's apparent high signing adoption rate is an illusion. Once platform-generated signatures are excluded, fewer than 6% of developers have ever signed a commit themselves, and the vast majority of apparent signers have never signed outside a web browser. Among the minority who do sign locally, signing rarely persists over time or across repositories, and roughly one in eight developer-managed signatures fails verification because signing keys are never uploaded to GitHub. Examining the key registry, we find that expired keys are almost never revoked and more than a quarter of users carry at least one dead key. Together, these findings reveal that commit signing as practiced today cannot serve as a dependable provenance signal at ecosystem scale, and we offer concrete recommendations for closing that gap.

</details>


### [41] [Comprehension Debt in GenAI-Assisted Software Engineering Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13277)
*Muhammad Ovais Ahmad*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了生成式AI工具在软件工程教育中如何导致理解债务积累，通过分析621篇学生反思日记，识别出四种积累模式和一种缓解模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI工具在软件开发中日益普及，尤其受到学生欢迎，因为它们能降低认知负荷。然而，这种采用也带来了社会认知风险：理解债务的积累。理解债务指的是开发团队对代码库的实际了解与有效维护和修改所需理解之间的差距。研究旨在探索GenAI工具如何在本科软件工程项目中促成理解债务的形成。

Method: 这是一项定性研究，基于207名学生在八周内撰写的621篇反思日记。研究分析了学生在软件工程项目中使用生成式AI工具（如ChatGPT、Claude）的情况，识别理解债务的积累模式。

Result: 研究识别出四种理解债务积累模式：1) AI作为黑盒代码接受；2) 上下文不匹配债务；3) 依赖诱导的萎缩；4) 验证绕过。同时发现一种缓解模式：学生将GenAI用作理解支架，帮助建立对代码的更深层次理解。研究强调理解债务与传统技术债务不同，它存在于开发团队的集体认知中而非代码库本身。

Conclusion: 理解债务是生成式AI工具采用带来的独特社会认知风险，需要明确的教学策略来缓解。建议在软件工程教育中强调验证实践、结构化回顾和主动学习评估，以帮助学生建立对代码的深入理解，而不仅仅是依赖AI生成解决方案。

Abstract: Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI) tools (e.g., ChatGPT, Calude) have rapidly become integral to software development. These tools are especially attractive to students, as they can reduce cognitive load. However, their adoption also introduces a socio-cognitive risk: the accumulation of Comprehension Debt (CD). CD refers to the growing gap between what a development team knows about its codebase and what it actually needs to understand in order to maintain and modify it effectively. This qualitative study investigate how GenAI tools contribute to CD in the context of an undergraduate software engineering project. Our study is based on 621 reflective diaries from 207 students over eight weeks. We identify four CD accumulation patterns and one mitigating pattern in students' use of GenAI tools. The four accumulation patterns include: (1) AI-as-black-box code acceptance, (2) context-mismatch debt, (3) dependency-induced atrophy, and (4) verification-bypass. In contrast, the mitigating pattern involves students using GenAI as a comprehension scaffold, allowing them to build a deeper understanding of the code. We argue that CD is distinct from traditional technical debt because it resides in the collective cognition of development teams rather than in the codebase itself. Our findings highlight the need for explicit pedagogical strategies to mitigate CD in software engineering education, emphasizing verification practices, structured retrospectives, and active learning assessments.

</details>


### [42] [The Cognitive Circuit Breaker: A Systems Engineering Framework for Intrinsic AI Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13417)
*Jonathan Pan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出Cognitive Circuit Breaker框架，通过提取LLM前向传播中的隐藏状态计算"认知失调差值"，实现低延迟的内在可靠性监控，检测幻觉和虚假真实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在关键任务系统中的部署增加，检测幻觉和"虚假真实性"成为重要工程挑战。现有可靠性架构依赖后生成的黑盒机制（如RAG交叉检查或LLM-as-a-judge评估器），这些外部方法引入不可接受的延迟、高计算开销和对外部API的依赖，经常违反软件工程SLA。

Method: 提出Cognitive Circuit Breaker系统工程框架，通过提取模型前向传播中的隐藏状态，计算"认知失调差值"——LLM外部语义置信度（softmax概率）与内部潜在确定性（通过线性探针推导）之间的数学差距。

Result: 展示了认知失调的统计显著检测，突出了架构相关的分布外泛化，并证明该框架对主动推理流程增加的计算开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: Cognitive Circuit Breaker提供了一种内在可靠性监控方法，相比现有外部方法具有显著延迟优势，适用于需要满足严格SLA的关键任务LLM部署场景。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in mission-critical software systems, detecting hallucinations and ``faked truthfulness'' has become a paramount engineering challenge. Current reliability architectures rely heavily on post-generation, black-box mechanisms, such as Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) cross-checking or LLM-as-a-judge evaluators. These extrinsic methods introduce unacceptable latency, high computational overhead, and reliance on secondary external API calls, frequently violating standard software engineering Service Level Agreements (SLAs). In this paper, we propose the Cognitive Circuit Breaker, a novel systems engineering framework that provides intrinsic reliability monitoring with minimal latency overhead. By extracting hidden states during a model's forward pass, we calculate the ``Cognitive Dissonance Delta'' -- the mathematical gap between an LLM's outward semantic confidence (softmax probabilities) and its internal latent certainty (derived via linear probes). We demonstrate statistically significant detection of cognitive dissonance, highlight architecture-dependent Out-of-Distribution (OOD) generalization, and show that this framework adds negligible computational overhead to the active inference pipeline.

</details>


### [43] [Learning from Change: Predictive Models for Incident Prevention in a Regulated IT Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13462)
*Eileen Kapel,Jan Lennartz,Luis Cruz,Diomidis Spinellis,Arie van Deursen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种用于大型国际银行的预测性事件风险评分方法，通过机器学习模型预测变更部署可能引发事件的风险，在满足监管要求的同时超越了基于规则的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在金融等高度监管的行业中，IT变更管理至关重要，因为大量IT事件由变更引起。需要识别高风险变更以确保运营可靠性、可审计性和可解释性，同时满足监管约束。

Method: 构建了具有可审计性和可解释性的预测模型，应用SHAP值提供特征级洞察。使用一年真实数据集，比较了现有基于规则的过程与三种机器学习模型：HGBC、LightGBM和XGBoost。LightGBM在增强聚合团队指标（捕捉组织上下文）时表现最佳。

Result: LightGBM模型取得了最佳性能，特别是当加入捕捉组织上下文的聚合团队指标时。数据驱动的可解释模型能够超越基于规则的方法，同时满足合规需求。

Conclusion: 研究表明，数据驱动的可解释模型可以超越基于规则的方法，同时满足合规需求，实现主动风险缓解和更可靠的IT运营。这种方法支持工程师在变更部署的评估和规划阶段预测潜在事件风险。

Abstract: Effective IT change management is important for businesses that depend on software and services, particularly in highly regulated sectors such as finance, where operational reliability, auditability, and explainability are essential. A significant portion of IT incidents are caused by changes, making it important to identify high-risk changes before deployment. This study presents a predictive incident risk scoring approach at a large international bank. The approach supports engineers during the assessment and planning phases of change deployments by predicting the potential of inducing incidents. To satisfy regulatory constraints, we built the model with auditability and explainability in mind, applying SHAP values to provide feature-level insights and ensure decisions are traceable and transparent. Using a one-year real-world dataset, we compare the existing rule-based process with three machine learning models: HGBC, LightGBM, and XGBoost. LightGBM achieved the best performance, particularly when enriched with aggregated team metrics that capture organisational context. Our results show that data-driven, interpretable models can outperform rule-based approaches while meeting compliance needs, enabling proactive risk mitigation and more reliable IT operations.

</details>


### [44] [From Exploration to Specification: LLM-Based Property Generation for Mobile App Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13463)
*Yiheng Xiong,Shiwen Song,Bo Ma,Ting Su,Xiaofei Xie*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: PropGen：一种自动化生成Android应用属性测试属性的方法，通过功能引导探索收集行为证据，合成属性，并基于测试反馈优化不精确属性，有效发现功能缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动应用存在大量不导致崩溃但表现为特定交互下错误行为的功能缺陷，这类缺陷难以自动检测，因为缺乏明确的测试预言。基于属性的测试能有效暴露这些缺陷，但依赖手动编写属性，成本高且困难，限制了其在移动应用中的实际应用。

Method: PropGen采用三阶段方法：1) 功能引导探索收集应用执行的行为证据；2) 从收集的证据中合成属性；3) 基于测试反馈优化不精确属性。该方法系统性地发现和执行应用功能，从观察到的行为中准确推导属性。

Result: 在12个真实Android应用上的评估显示：PropGen识别了1,210个有效功能并正确执行了977个（基线为491和187）；生成了985个属性，其中912个有效；修复了127个不精确属性中的118个。使用生成的属性发现了25个先前未知的功能缺陷，其中许多被现有功能测试技术遗漏。

Conclusion: PropGen能够有效识别和执行应用功能，生成有效属性并修复不精确属性，显著提升了基于属性测试在移动应用中的实用性，成功发现了现有技术遗漏的功能缺陷。

Abstract: Mobile apps often suffer from functional bugs that do not cause crashes but instead manifest as incorrect behaviors under specific user interactions. Such bugs are difficult to detect automatically because they often lack explicit test oracles. Property-based testing can effectively expose them by checking intended behavioral properties under diverse interactions. However, its use largely depends on manually written properties, whose construction is difficult and expensive, limiting its practical use for mobile apps.
  To address this limitation, we propose PropGen, an automated approach for generating properties for Android apps. However, this task is challenging for two reasons: app functionalities are often hard to systematically uncover and execute, and properties are difficult to derive accurately from observed behaviors. To this end, PropGen performs functionality-guided exploration to collect behavioral evidence from app executions, synthesizes properties from the collected evidence, and refines imprecise properties based on testing feedback. We implemented PropGen and evaluated it on 12 real-world Android apps. The results show that PropGen can effectively identify and execute valid app functionalities, generate valid properties, and repair most imprecise ones. Across all apps, PropGen identified 1,210 valid functionalities and correctly executed 977 of them, compared with 491 and 187 for the baseline. It generated 985 properties, 912 of which were valid, and repaired 118 of 127 imprecise ones exposed during testing. With the resulting properties, we found 25 previously unknown functional bugs in the latest versions of the subject apps, many of which were missed by existing functional testing techniques.

</details>


### [45] [TORAI: Unsupervised Fine-grained RCA using Multi-Source Telemetry Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13522)
*Luan Pham,Huong Ha,Xiuzhen Zhang,Hongyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TORAI：一种无需服务调用图的无监督多源根因分析方法，通过异常严重度测量、聚类和因果分析处理微服务系统中的盲点服务问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多源根因分析方法假设所有服务都有追踪数据来构建服务调用图，但在实际微服务系统中存在没有追踪数据的盲点服务（如编译软件或不支持的服务），这会影响现有方法的性能，因为它们只能诊断调用图上可见的服务。

Method: TORAI首先使用可用的多源遥测数据测量异常严重度，然后基于严重度症状对服务进行聚类，在每个严重度簇内进行因果分析以对服务进行排序，最后聚合簇排名并使用假设检验识别细粒度根因。

Result: 在三个基准系统上的实验表明，TORAI在存在盲点的情况下显著优于最先进的基线方法。在实际故障上的性能进一步显示，TORAI能够在top-3推荐中准确识别根因。

Conclusion: TORAI提供了一种无监督方法，利用可用的多源遥测数据进行根因分析，无需构建服务调用图或进一步的侵入性操作，从而解决了现有方法的局限性。

Abstract: Existing multi-source root cause analysis (RCA) methods for microservice systems assume all services have traces to construct a service call graph. However, this assumption is not practical as microservice systems evolve rapidly and may contain blackbox services without traces, such as compiled software or unsupported services. We refer to these services as blind spots. In the presence of blind spots, the performance of existing multi-source RCA methods may be affected, as they only diagnose visible services on the call graph. To overcome this limitation, we propose TORAI, a novel unsupervised approach that effectively pinpoints fine-grained root causes without relying on the service call graph. Instead, TORAI first measures anomaly severity using available multi-source telemetry data. It then performs clustering to group services based on their severity symptoms and conducts causal analysis to rank services within each severity cluster. Finally, TORAI aggregates the cluster rankings and uses hypothesis testing to identify fine-grained root causes. TORAI provides an unsupervised approach that leverages available multi-source telemetry data for RCA without requiring a constructed service call graph or further intrusive actions, thus addressing the limitations of existing methods. Our experiments on three benchmark systems demonstrate that TORAI outperforms state-of-the-art baselines remarkably in the presence of blind spots. Performance on real-world failures further shows that TORAI can accurately pinpoint the root causes in top-3 recommendations.

</details>


### [46] [WebMAC: A Multi-Agent Collaborative Framework for Scenario Testing of Web Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13559)
*Zhenyu Wan,Gong Chen,Qing Huang,Xiaoyuan Xie*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: WebMAC是一个用于Web系统场景测试的多智能体协作框架，通过交互式澄清补全测试场景描述，并基于等价类划分生成充分实例化测试脚本，显著提升测试脚本执行成功率和错误检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的场景测试方法存在两个主要局限：1）受限于自然语言描述的不完整性；2）忽视测试充分性准则，导致难以检测潜在错误。需要一种能自动补全测试场景描述并确保测试充分性的方法。

Method: 提出WebMAC多智能体协作框架，包含三个模块：1）通过交互式澄清补全测试场景自然语言描述；2）基于等价类划分将测试场景转化为充分实例化形式；3）将实例化场景转换为可执行测试脚本。采用多智能体协同工作流程。

Result: 在四个Web系统上的评估显示：相比SOTA方法，WebMAC将生成测试脚本的执行成功率提升30%-60%，测试效率提高29%，token消耗减少47.6%。能有效检测更多Web系统错误。

Conclusion: WebMAC通过多智能体协作有效解决了现有LLM场景测试方法的局限性，显著提升了测试脚本质量、执行成功率和错误检测能力，同时降低了计算资源消耗。

Abstract: Scenario testing is an important technique for detecting errors in web systems. Testers draft test scenarios and convert them into test scripts for execution. Early methods relied on testers to convert test scenarios into test scripts. Recent LLM-based scenario testing methods can generate test scripts from natural language descriptions of test scenarios. However, these methods are not only limited by the incompleteness of descriptions but also overlook test adequacy criteria, making it difficult to detect potential errors. To address these limitations, this paper proposes WebMAC, a multi-agent collaborative framework for scenario testing of web systems. WebMAC can complete natural language descriptions of test scenarios through interactive clarification and transform adequate instantiated test scenarios via equivalence class partitioning. WebMAC consists of three multi-agent modules, responsible respectively for completing natural language descriptions of test scenarios, transforming test scenarios, and converting test scripts. We evaluated WebMAC on four web systems. Compared with the SOTA method, WebMAC improves the execution success rate of generated test scripts by 30%-60%, increases testing efficiency by 29%, and reduces token consumption by 47.6%. Furthermore, WebMAC can effectively detect more errors in web systems.

</details>


### [47] [V2E: Validating Smart Contract Vulnerabilities through Profit-driven Exploit Generation and Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13611)
*Jingwen Zhang,Yuhong Nan,Kaiwen Ning,Mingxi Ye,Wei Li,Yuming Xiao,Yuming Feng,Weizhe Zhang,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: V2E是一个用于验证智能合约漏洞是否真正可利用的框架，通过自动生成可执行的PoC并评估其触发性和盈利能力来减少误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能合约漏洞检测工具存在高误报率问题，导致开发者和审计人员需要大量手动验证。主要原因是报告的漏洞虽然符合特定模式，但可能实际上不可利用，因为漏洞代码无法触发或不会造成实际财务损失。

Method: V2E通过PoC生成、验证和细化的组合自动化整个过程：1) 通过分析潜在漏洞路径生成针对性PoC；2) 通过触发性和盈利能力分析验证PoC有效性；3) 基于PoC执行反馈迭代细化PoC，增加确认漏洞的机会。

Result: 在264个手动标记的合约上的评估显示，V2E优于基线方法。

Conclusion: V2E框架能够有效验证智能合约漏洞的可利用性，通过自动生成和验证PoC来减少误报，提高漏洞检测的准确性和实用性。

Abstract: Smart contracts are a critical component of blockchain systems. Due to the large amount of digital assets carried by smart contracts, their security is of critical importance. Although numerous tools have been developed for detecting smart contract vulnerability, their effectiveness remains limited, particularly due to the high false positives included in the reported results. Therefore, developers and auditors are often overwhelmed with manually verifying the reported issues. A fundamental reason behind this is that while a reported vulnerability satisfies specific vulnerable patterns, it may not actually be exploitable, either because the vulnerable code cannot be triggered or it does not result in any financial loss.
  In this paper, we propose V2E, a new framework for validating whether a reported vulnerability is truly exploitable. The core idea of V2E is to automatically generate executable Proof-of-Concept Exploit (PoC for short), and then assess if the vulnerability could be triggered and incur any real damage (i.e., causing financial loss) by the PoC. While LLMs have shown proficiency in PoC generation, achieving our task is by no means trivial. In detail, it is difficult for LLM to: (1) generate and update PoC to trigger a specific vulnerability, (2) evaluate the PoC's effectiveness to validate exploitable vulnerability. To this end, V2E automates the whole process through a novel combination of PoC generation, validation, and refinement: (1) Firstly, V2E generates targeted PoCs by analyzing potential vulnerability paths. (2) Then, V2E verifies the validity of PoCs through triggerability and profitability analysis. (3) In addition, V2E iteratively refines the generated PoC based on PoC execution feedback, therefore, increasing the chance to confirm the vulnerability. Evaluation on 264 manually labeled contracts shows that V2E outperforms the baseline approach.

</details>


### [48] [Figma2Code: Automating Multimodal Design to Code in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13648)
*Yi Gui,Jiawan Zhang,Yina Wang,Tianran Ma,Yao Wan,Shilin He,Dongping Chen,Zhou Zhao,Wenbin Jiang,Xuanhua Shi,Hai Jin,Philip S Yu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Figma2Code：将Figma设计文件转换为前端代码的多模态任务，利用Figma元数据提升UI代码生成质量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有设计转代码方法仅依赖设计图像，需从图像推断复杂UI细节，导致结果质量下降。实际开发中设计稿通常以Figma文件形式交付，包含丰富的元数据和资源信息，对生成高质量UI代码至关重要。

Method: 提出Figma2Code任务，收集Figma社区的设计图像和对应元数据文件，通过规则过滤、人工和MLLM标注筛选、元数据精炼等处理流程，构建包含3,055个样本的数据集，从中精选213个高质量案例作为平衡数据集，并基于此基准测试10个开源和专有MLLM。

Result: 专有模型在视觉保真度上表现优越，但在布局响应性和代码可维护性方面仍有局限。跨模态和消融实验证实了这一限制，部分原因是模型倾向于直接从Figma元数据映射原始视觉属性。

Conclusion: Figma2Code任务将设计转代码推进到多模态场景，揭示了当前MLLM在利用Figma元数据生成高质量、响应式、可维护UI代码方面的局限性，为实际开发工作流中的自动化设计转代码提供了新基准。

Abstract: Front-end development constitutes a substantial portion of software engineering, yet converting design mockups into production-ready User Interface (UI) code remains tedious and costly. While recent work has explored automating this process with Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), existing approaches typically rely solely on design images. As a result, they must infer complex UI details from images alone, often leading to degraded results. In real-world development workflows, however, design mockups are usually delivered as Figma files, a widely used tool for front-end design, that embed rich multimodal information (e.g., metadata and assets) essential for generating high-quality UI. To bridge this gap, we introduce Figma2Code, a new task that advances design-to-code into a multimodal setting and aims to automate design-to-code in the wild. Specifically, we collect paired design images and their corresponding metadata files from the Figma community. We then apply a series of processing operations, including rule-based filtering, human- and MLLM-based annotation and screening, and metadata refinement. This process yields 3,055 samples, from which designers curate a balanced dataset of 213 high-quality cases. Using this dataset, we benchmark ten state-of-the-art open-source and proprietary MLLMs. Our results show that while proprietary models achieve superior visual fidelity, they remain limited in layout responsiveness and code maintainability. Further experiments across modalities and ablation studies corroborate this limitation, partly due to models' tendency to directly map primitive visual attributes from Figma metadata.

</details>


### [49] [Debugging Performance Issues in WebAssembly Runtimes via Mutation-based Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13693)
*Ruiying Zeng,Shuyao Jiang,Wenxuan Zhao,Yangfan Zhou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: WarpL是一个基于突变的性能调试工具，用于识别WebAssembly运行时中导致性能问题的次优指令序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WebAssembly运行时中的性能问题会显著降低托管服务能力，现有针对应用级低效的调试方法不适用于Wasm运行时中的编译次优问题。

Method: WarpL采用基于突变的方法：获取功能相似但性能问题不出现的突变体，通过比较原始程序和突变程序的机器代码来隔离次优指令序列。

Result: 在三个广泛使用的Wasm运行时的12个真实性能问题中，WarpL成功识别了10个问题的确切原因，并成功诊断了Wasmtime中的6个先前未知性能问题。

Conclusion: WarpL是一种有效的性能调试方法，能够精确识别Wasm运行时中的次优编译指令序列，有助于缩小根本原因范围。

Abstract: Performance debugging in WebAssembly (Wasm) runtimes is essential for ensuring the robustness of Wasm, especially since performance issues have frequently occurred in Wasm runtimes, which can significantly degrade the capabilities of hosted services. Many performance issues in Wasm runtimes result from suboptimal compilation of input Wasm programs, for which existing performance debugging methods primarily designed for application-level inefficiencies are not well-suited. In this paper, we present WarpL, a novel mutation-based approach that aims to identify the exact suboptimal instruction sequences responsible for the performance issues in Wasm runtimes, thereby narrowing down the root causes. Specifically, WarpL obtains a functionally similar mutant in which the performance issue does not manifest, and isolates the exact suboptimal instructions by comparing the machine code of the original and mutated programs. We implement WarpL as an open-source tool and evaluate it on 12 real-world performance issues across three widely used Wasm runtimes. WarpL identified the exact causes in 10 out of 12 issues. Notably, we have used WarpL to successfully diagnose six previously unknown performance issues in Wasmtime.

</details>


### [50] [On the Effectiveness of Context Compression for Repository-Level Tasks: An Empirical Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13725)
*Jia Feng,Zhanyue Qin,Cuiyun Gao,Ruiqi Wang,Chaozheng Wang,Yingwei Ma,Xiaoyuan Xie*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文首次系统研究了代码仓库级智能任务中的上下文压缩技术，将八种方法分为三类范式，证明在4倍压缩下连续潜在向量方法性能提升28.3%，所有范式都能显著降低推理延迟和成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码仓库级智能任务需要处理长多文件上下文，这带来三个挑战：关键信息被噪声掩盖、因窗口限制被截断、推理延迟增加。上下文压缩可以缓解这些问题，但在代码任务中的应用尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 将八种上下文压缩方法组织为三类范式：离散标记序列、连续潜在向量和视觉标记。在代码补全和生成任务上评估这些方法，测量性能和效率指标。

Result: 在4倍压缩下，连续潜在向量方法比完整上下文性能提升高达28.3%（BLEU分数），表明它们过滤了噪声而不仅仅是截断。所有范式都降低了推理成本，视觉和文本压缩在高压缩比下可将端到端延迟降低50%，接近无仓库上下文的推理成本。

Conclusion: 上下文压缩是代码仓库级智能任务的有效方法，研究结果为范式选择提供了指导，证明了压缩不仅能减少计算成本，还能通过过滤噪声提升性能。

Abstract: Repository-level code intelligence tasks require large language models (LLMs) to process long, multi-file contexts. Such inputs introduce three challenges: crucial context can be obscured by noise, truncated due to limited windows, and increased inference latency. Context compression mitigates these risks by condensing inputs. While studied in NLP, its applicability to code tasks remains largely unexplored. We present the first systematic empirical study of context compression for repository-level code intelligence, organizing eight methods into three paradigms: discrete token sequences, continuous latent vectors, and visual tokens. We evaluate them on code completion and generation, measuring performance and efficiency. Results show context compression is effective: at 4x compression, continuous latent vector methods surpass full-context performance by up to 28.3% in BLEU score, indicating they filter noise rather than just truncating. On efficiency, all paradigms reduce inference cost. Both visual and text-based compression achieve up to 50% reduction in end-to-end latency at high ratios, approaching the cost of inference without repository context. These findings establish context compression as a viable approach and provide guidance for paradigm selection.

</details>


### [51] [A Universal Textual Merge Strategy Based on Tokens for Version Control Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13813)
*Qiqi Jason Gu,Mikoláš Janota*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Summer是一种基于文本令牌的合并算法，独立于文档格式，通过令牌级变化建模为字符串重写规则和移动规则，在真实合并场景中实现了36%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于行的合并算法在重构或并行编辑时容易产生虚假冲突，而语法和语义感知的合并方法存在格式丢失、依赖语言特定解析器、跨异构工件灵活性有限等问题。

Method: 将文本划分为令牌，将分支中的令牌级变化建模为字符串重写规则和移动规则，然后将这些规则应用于另一个分支的文本以构建合并。移动规则可以模拟函数提取和内联操作。

Result: 在ConflictBench（大型真实合并场景基准）上评估，与五种先进合并工具在Java和非Java文件上比较。Summer实现了36%的最高准确率（与开发者合并结果完全一致），在语义准确率方面排名第二。

Conclusion: Summer提出了一种独立于编程语言的文本令牌合并方法，有效减少了虚假冲突，在真实合并场景中表现出色，平衡了准确性和通用性。

Abstract: Merging is a core operation in version control systems such as Git, but traditional line-based algorithms often yield spurious conflicts, particularly in the presence of refactorings or parallel edits. While syntax- and semantics-aware merging approaches can reduce conflicts, they introduce drawbacks such as loss of formatting, dependence on language-specific parsers, and limited flexibility across heterogeneous artifacts. To address this gap, we present Summer, a novel textual token-based merge algorithm independent of document formats. Dividing text into tokens, our approach formulates token-level changes in one branch into string-rewriting rules and move rules, and applies these rules to the text of the other branch to construct a merge. Despite being independent on programming languages, our move rules model extracting and inlining functions. We evaluated Summer on ConflictBench, a large benchmark of real-world merge scenarios, comparing it with five pioneering merge tools across Java and non-Java files. Experimental results show that Summer achieved the highest 36% accuracy in reproducing merges verbatim identical to developers', and ranked second in semantic accuracy.

</details>


### [52] [Sentiment analysis for software engineering: How far can zero-shot learning (ZSL) go?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13826)
*Reem Alfayez,Manal Binkhonain*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究探索零样本学习在软件工程情感分析中的应用潜力，以解决标注数据稀缺问题，发现特定ZSL技术能达到与微调Transformer模型相当的F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程领域情感分析需要专门工具，但开发这些工具依赖监督学习所需的标注数据集，而获取此类数据集面临领域专业知识需求和巨大工作量挑战。

Method: 通过实证实验评估多种ZSL技术（嵌入基、NLI基、TARS基、生成基），在不同标签设置下考察性能，与微调Transformer模型对比，并进行错误分析识别误分类主要原因。

Result: 研究发现，结合专家策划标签的嵌入基或生成基ZSL技术能达到与微调Transformer模型相当的宏F1分数。错误分析显示标注主观性和极性事实是ZSL误分类的主要因素。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了ZSL在软件工程情感分析中的潜力，通过减少对标注数据的依赖，为解决标注数据集稀缺挑战提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Sentiment analysis in software engineering focuses on understanding emotions expressed in software artifacts. Previous research highlighted the limitations of applying general off-the-shelf sentiment analysis tools within the software engineering domain and indicated the need for specialized tools tailored to various software engineering contexts. The development of such tools heavily relies on supervised machine learning techniques that necessitate annotated datasets. Acquiring such datasets is a substantial challenge, as it requires domain-specific expertise and significant effort. Objective: This study explores the potential of ZSL to address the scarcity of annotated datasets in sentiment analysis within software engineering Method:} We conducted an empirical experiment to evaluate the performance of various ZSL techniques, including embedding-based, NLI-based, TARS-based, and generative-based ZSL techniques. We assessed the performance of these techniques under different labels setups to examine the impact of label configurations. Additionally, we compared the results of the ZSL techniques with state-of-the-art fine-tuned transformer-based models. Finally, we performed an error analysis to identify the primary causes of misclassifications. Results: Our findings demonstrate that ZSL techniques, particularly those combining expert-curated labels with embedding-based or generative-based models, can achieve macro-F1 scores comparable to fine-tuned transformer-based models. The error analysis revealed that subjectivity in annotation and polar facts are the main contributors to ZSL misclassifications. Conclusion: This study demonstrates the potential of ZSL for sentiment analysis in software engineering. ZSL can provide a solution to the challenge of annotated dataset scarcity by reducing reliance on annotated dataset.

</details>


### [53] [Towards Enabling An Artificial Self-Construction Software Life-cycle via Autopoietic Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13934)
*Daniel Rodriguez-Cardenas,David Nader Palacio,Denys Poshyvanyk*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出基于自创生架构的软件自构造新范式，利用基础模型实现软件自主进化与维护


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统软件维护成本高且依赖人工，基础模型的出现为软件自主行为提供了新机遇，需要从根本上改变软件开发生命周期

Method: 提出自创生架构（特别是Psi-Arch）作为软件自构造的基础框架，整合基础模型推理单元，建立新的架构范式

Result: 本文为立场论文，未提供具体解决方案，但识别了传统维护方法的局限性，并提出了实现软件自构造的核心挑战

Conclusion: 软件自构造代表了SDLC自动化的新前沿，需要推动相关研究讨论，实现软件开发范式的根本转变

Abstract: Software engineering research has focused on automating maintenance and evolution processes to reduce costs and improve reliability. The emergence of foundation models (FMs) with strong code understanding and reasoning abilities offers new opportunities for autonomous software behavior. Inspired by Artificial Life (ALife), we propose a fundamental shift in the Software Development Life-Cycle (SDLC) by introducing self-construction mechanisms that enable software to evolve and maintain autonomously. This position paper explores the potential of Autopoietic Architectures, specifically Psi-Arch, as a foundational framework for self-constructing software. We first analyze the limitations of traditional maintenance approaches and identify gaps in current SDLC automation. Subsequently, we outline the core challenges in achieving self-construction, including the integration of foundation-model-based reasoning units and the establishment of novel architectural paradigms. Although this paper does not present a definitive solution, it seeks to catalyze discourse and inspire research toward a new paradigm in software engineering, one in which self-constructing software represents the next frontier in SDLC automation.

</details>


### [54] [CollabCoder: Plan-Code Co-Evolution via Collaborative Decision-Making for Efficient Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13946)
*Duy Tung Doan,Quang Huy Phung,Dzung Nguyen,Khac-Hoai Nam Bui*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CollabCoder：一种新颖的Plan-Code协同进化框架，通过动态多智能体协作提升代码生成质量，在降低计算开销的同时实现优于现有方法的性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多智能体框架在代码生成中存在静态规划、孤立执行、高计算开销和复杂任务适应性有限等问题，需要更高效的协作机制

Method: 提出Plan-Code协同进化框架，设计规划模块和代码模块之间的协作决策过程，动态决定哪个模块执行调试过程

Result: 在广泛使用的基准测试中，CollabCoder持续提升代码质量和鲁棒性，在LiveCodeBench和xCodeEval等更具挑战性的基准上性能提升11-20%，同时平均减少4-10次API调用

Conclusion: CollabCoder在保持或超越当前最先进方法性能的同时显著降低计算开销，随着基准难度增加效率优势更加明显，为自动化代码生成提供了更高效的解决方案

Abstract: Automated code generation remains a persistent challenge in software engineering, as conventional multi-agent frameworks are often constrained by static planning, isolated execution, high computational overhead, and limited adaptability to complex tasks. This paper introduces CollabCoder, a novel Plan-Code Co-Evolution framework that improves code generation through dynamic multi-agent collaboration. The core idea is to design a collaborative decision-making process between the plan module and the code module to decide which module should be executed for the debugging process. Extensive experiments on widely used benchmarks demonstrate that CollabCoder consistently improves code quality and robustness across tasks. Importantly, CollabCoder achieves performance comparable to or exceeding current state-of-the-art methods while reducing computational overhead, with efficiency gains becoming more pronounced as benchmark difficulty increases. On the more challenging LiveCodeBench and xCodeEval benchmarks, our approach improves performance by 11-20% over strong baselines while reducing the number of API calls by an average of 4-10 per execution.

</details>


### [55] [Learned or Memorized ? Quantifying Memorization Advantage in Code LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13997)
*Djiré Albérick Euraste,Kaboré Abdoul Kader,Jordan Samhi,Earl T. Barr,Jacques Klein,Tegawendé F. Bissyandé*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出基于扰动的记忆优势量化方法，评估8个开源代码大语言模型在19个基准测试中的记忆化风险，发现记忆化程度因模型和任务而异，并揭示CVEFixes和Defects4J等基准测试的记忆化风险低于预期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于缺乏代码数据集透明度，难以检测、评估和缓解数据泄露问题。需要量化代码大语言模型的记忆优势，以理解模型在训练数据上的记忆化程度。

Method: 提出基于扰动的记忆优势量化方法，定义模型在可能见过和未见输入上的性能差距。评估8个开源代码大语言模型在19个基准测试上，涵盖代码生成、代码理解、漏洞检测和bug修复四个任务家族。

Result: 不同模型和任务表现出广泛敏感性差异：StarCoder在某些基准上敏感性高达0.8，而QwenCoder大多低于0.4。代码摘要任务敏感性较低，测试生成任务敏感性较高。CVEFixes和Defects4J两个基准测试的记忆化优势较低（CVEFixes低于0.1），表明模型更多依赖学习到的泛化能力而非直接记忆。

Conclusion: 记忆化风险高度依赖于任务和模型，需要更强的评估协议，特别是在安全关键场景中。研究为理解代码大语言模型的记忆化行为提供了实证证据。

Abstract: The lack of transparency about code datasets used to train large language models (LLMs) makes it difficult to detect, evaluate, and mitigate data leakage. We present a perturbation-based method to quantify memorization advantage in code LLMs, defined as the performance gap between likely seen and unseen inputs.
  We evaluate 8 open-source code LLMs on 19 benchmarks across four task families: code generation, code understanding, vulnerability detection, and bug fixing. Sensitivity patterns vary widely across models and tasks. For example, StarCoder reaches high sensitivity on some benchmarks (up to 0.8), while QwenCoder remains lower (mostly below 0.4), suggesting differences in generalization behavior. Task categories also differ: code summarization tends to show low sensitivity, whereas test generation is substantially higher.
  We then analyze two widely discussed benchmarks, CVEFixes and Defects4J, often suspected of leakage. Contrary to common concerns, both show low memorization advantage across models: CVEFixes remains below 0.1, and Defects4J is lower than other program repair benchmarks. These results suggest that, for these datasets, models may rely more on learned generalization than direct memorization.
  Overall, our findings provide evidence that memorization risk is highly task- and model-dependent, and highlight the need for stronger evaluation protocols, especially in security-focused settings.

</details>


### [56] [Large Language Models to Enhance Business Process Modeling: Past, Present, and Future Trends](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14034)
*João Bettencourt,Sérgio Guerreiro*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对AI驱动的方法将自然语言转换为BPMN流程模型的文献进行综述，重点关注大语言模型的作用，揭示了从传统方法向LLM架构的转变趋势，并识别了现有挑战和研究空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式人工智能特别是大语言模型的发展，人们对使用自然语言自动化或辅助业务流程建模任务的兴趣日益增长。然而，这些方法在组织环境中有效支持复杂流程建模的程度尚不明确，需要系统梳理现有研究进展。

Method: 采用结构化综述策略，识别和分析相关研究，对现有方法进行分类，考察LLM如何集成到文本到模型管道中，并调查用于评估生成模型的实践方法。

Result: 分析显示从基于规则和传统NLP管道向LLM架构的明显转变，这些架构依赖提示工程、中间表示和迭代精炼机制。虽然显著扩展了自动化流程模型生成能力，但也暴露了语义正确性、评估碎片化、可重复性以及在真实组织环境中验证有限等持续挑战。

Conclusion: 基于研究发现，本文识别了关键研究空白并讨论了未来研究方向，包括通过检索增强生成集成上下文知识、与LLM的整合、交互式建模架构的开发，以及更全面标准化评估框架的需求。

Abstract: Recent advances in Generative Artificial Intelligence, particularly Large Language Models (LLMs), have stimulated growing interest in automating or assisting Business Process Modeling tasks using natural language. Several approaches have been proposed to transform textual process descriptions into BPMN and related workflow models. However, the extent to which these approaches effectively support complex process modeling in organizational settings remains unclear. This article presents a literature review of AI-driven methods for transforming natural language into BPMN process models, with a particular focus on the role of LLMs. Following a structured review strategy, relevant studies were identified and analyzed to classify existing approaches, examine how LLMs are integrated into text-to-model pipelines, and investigate the evaluation practices used to assess generated models. The analysis reveals a clear shift from rule-based and traditional NLP pipelines toward LLM-based architectures that rely on prompt engineering, intermediate representations, and iterative refinement mechanisms. While these approaches significantly expand the capabilities of automated process model generation, the literature also exposes persistent challenges related to semantic correctness, evaluation fragmentation, reproducibility, and limited validation in real-world organizational contexts. Based on these findings, this review identifies key research gaps and discusses promising directions for future research, including the integration of contextual knowledge through Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), its integration with LLMs, the development of interactive modeling architectures, and the need for more comprehensive and standardized evaluation frameworks.

</details>

### [57] [Learning Probabilistic Responsibility Allocations for Multi-Agent Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13128)
*Isaac Remy,Caleb Chang,Karen Leung*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于条件变分自编码器学习多智能体交互中责任分配概率模型的方法，通过可微分优化层将责任分配映射到可观测的控制信号，在驾驶数据集上验证了预测性能和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类交互行为不仅受个体目标影响，还受与他人共享的约束（如安全）影响。理解人们如何分配责任（即个体为适应他人而偏离其期望策略的程度）对于设计社会合规且可信的自主系统至关重要。

Method: 结合条件变分自编码器的潜在空间和多智能体轨迹预测技术，学习基于场景和智能体上下文的责任分配概率分布。通过可微分优化层将责任分配映射到诱导控制信号，解决了缺乏真实责任标签的问题。

Result: 在INTERACTION驾驶数据集上评估，方法不仅实现了强大的预测性能，还通过责任视角为多智能体交互模式提供了可解释的洞察。

Conclusion: 提出的概率责任分配模型能够捕捉多智能体交互中的多模态不确定性，为理解人类交互行为和设计社会合规的自主系统提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Human behavior in interactive settings is shaped not only by individual objectives but also by shared constraints with others, such as safety. Understanding how people allocate responsibility, i.e., how much one deviates from their desired policy to accommodate others, can inform the design of socially compliant and trustworthy autonomous systems. In this work, we introduce a method for learning a probabilistic responsibility allocation model that captures the multimodal uncertainty inherent in multi-agent interactions. Specifically, our approach leverages the latent space of a conditional variational autoencoder, combined with techniques from multi-agent trajectory forecasting, to learn a distribution over responsibility allocations conditioned on scene and agent context. Although ground-truth responsibility labels are unavailable, the model remains tractable by incorporating a differentiable optimization layer that maps responsibility allocations to induced controls, which are available. We evaluate our method on the INTERACTION driving dataset and demonstrate that it not only achieves strong predictive performance but also provides interpretable insights, through the lens of responsibility, into patterns of multi-agent interaction.

</details>


### [58] [MIND: AI Co-Scientist for Material Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13699)
*Geonhee Ahn,Donghyun Lee,Hayoung Doo,Jonggeol Na,Hyunsoo Cho,Sookyung Kim*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: MIND是一个基于大语言模型的自动化材料研究假设验证框架，通过多智能体流程实现假设精炼、实验和辩论式验证，集成了机器学习原子间势能进行硅基实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的科学发现系统大多局限于文本推理，缺乏自动化实验验证能力，需要开发能够将假设生成与实验验证相结合的系统。

Method: 采用多智能体管道组织科学发现过程，包括假设精炼、实验和基于辩论的验证；集成机器学习原子间势能（特别是SevenNet-Omni）进行可扩展的硅基实验；提供基于Web的用户界面进行自动化假设测试。

Result: 开发了MIND框架，实现了自动化假设验证系统；提供了开源代码和演示视频；模块化设计允许集成更多实验模块，适应更广泛的科学工作流程。

Conclusion: MIND框架成功将大语言模型驱动的假设生成与自动化实验验证相结合，为材料研究提供了端到端的科学发现系统，其模块化设计使其具有扩展到其他科学领域的潜力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have enabled agentic AI systems for scientific discovery, but most approaches remain limited to textbased reasoning without automated experimental verification. We propose MIND, an LLM-driven framework for automated hypothesis validation in materials research. MIND organizes the scientific discovery process into hypothesis refinement, experimentation, and debate-based validation within a multi-agent pipeline. For experimental verification, the system integrates Machine Learning Interatomic Potentials, particularly SevenNet-Omni, enabling scalable in-silico experiments. We also provide a web-based user interface for automated hypothesis testing. The modular design allows additional experimental modules to be integrated, making the framework adaptable to broader scientific workflows. The code is available at: https://github.com/IMMS-Ewha/MIND, and a demonstration video at: https://youtu.be/lqiFe1OQzN4.

</details>

### [59] [DTCO Exploration of NOR-Type IGZO FeFETs for Read-Dominated Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13624)
*Yang Xiang,Zhuo Chen,Nicolo Ronchi,Arvind Sharma,Fernando Garcia-Redondo,Subhali Subhechha,Attilio Belmonte,Maarten Rosmeulen,Gouri Sankar Kar,Dwaipayan Biswas,Jan Van Houdt*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文评估了IGZO FeFET NOR型存储器在AI推理工作负载中的可行性，通过设计-技术协同优化展示了其跨节点位元面积可扩展性、亚5ns访问延迟，并分析了负阈值电压导致的漏电流问题及3D堆叠限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IGZO FeFET因其优异的耐久性而受到关注，本文旨在评估NOR型IGZO FeFET在面向AI推理的读取密集型工作负载中的可行性，通过设计-技术协同优化解决实际应用中的挑战。

Method: 采用设计-技术协同优化方法，评估IGZO FeFET的跨节点位元面积可扩展性，分析BEOL工艺下的面积优势，研究负阈值电压导致的漏电流问题及其解决方案（如铁电层减薄），并探讨3D堆叠中的漏电流限制。

Result: 实现了7nm工艺规则下0.016μm²的SRAM等效面积，达到亚5ns随机访问延迟；识别了负阈值电压导致的漏电流对读取裕量的影响，提出了正阈值电压工程解决方案；阐明了3D堆叠中邻接通道分流导致的漏电流对存储级存储器密度限制。

Conclusion: IGZO FeFET NOR型存储器在AI推理应用中具有潜力，通过正阈值电压工程可解决漏电流问题，但3D堆叠密度受限于邻接通道分流效应，需要进一步优化以实现存储级存储器应用。

Abstract: InGaZnO (IGZO) channel FeFETs have attracted notable interest thanks to their advances in endurance. This work evaluates the viability of NOR-type IGZO FeFETs for readcentric AI inference workloads via design-technology cooptimization (DTCO). We demonstrate the cross-node bitcell footprint scalability of back-end-of-line (BEOL) IGZO FeFETs capable of delivering 10-A SRAM-equivalent area (0.016 um2) with 7-nm ground rules and reaching sub-5 ns random access latency despite writability challenges. We further identify the sensing margin penalty in NOR FeFET arrays arising from sneak current associated with the negative program-state Vt, which requires positive-Vt engineering in order to eliminate the unwanted negative voltage read inhibition - for example, by ferroelectric layer thinning. Last but not least, we elucidate the read margin implications on 3D FeNOR for storage-class memories (SCMs), with the 3D stacking density limited by additional sneak current from neighbor channel shunting.

</details>

### [60] [Autoencoder-Based CSI Compression for Beyond Wi-Fi 8 Coordinated Beamforming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13500)
*Ibrahim Aboushehada,Boris Bellalta,Giovanni Geraci,Lorenzo Galati Giordano*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于自动编码器的CSI压缩机制，集成到IEEE 802.11bn MAC设计中，用于减少密集Wi-Fi部署中协调波束成形所需的CSI反馈开销，从而提高多接入点协调的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集Wi-Fi部署中的协调波束成形需要大量CSI反馈，通过重叠基本服务集进行信道探测会产生高开销，这限制了多接入点协调的性能。现有IEEE 802.11 CSI压缩方法仍导致高信道探测开销，在某些情况下甚至不如传统传输。

Method: 提出基于自动编码器的CSI压缩机制，集成到IEEE 802.11bn MAC设计中。使用Sionna RT生成真实信道，通过事件驱动模拟器评估AE重建精度与反馈大小之间的权衡，测量它们对信道探测开销和数据延迟的影响。

Result: AE压缩相比IEEE 802.11 CSI压缩减少50%以上信道探测开销，1/4压缩比提供最佳精度/反馈大小权衡，实现最低数据延迟。AE压缩使协调波束成形性能优于传统传输，在吞吐量和数据延迟方面获得显著增益。

Conclusion: 基于自动编码器的CSI压缩是未来Wi-Fi系统中实现高效多接入点协调操作的有前景技术，能够显著减少反馈开销，改善协调波束成形性能，克服现有压缩方法的局限性。

Abstract: Coordinated beamforming (Co-BF) is a key multi-access-point coordination (MAPC) technique for dense Wi-Fi deployments, but its performance can be hindered by the large channel state information (CSI) feedback required through channel sounding across overlapping basic service sets (OBSS). This work proposes an autoencoder (AE)-based CSI compression mechanism integrated into a standards-aligned IEEE 802.11bn MAC design. Using an event-driven simulator with realistic channels generated through Sionna RT, we evaluate the tradeoff between AE reconstruction accuracy and feedback size by measuring their impact on channel sounding overhead and data latency. Our results show that AE-based compression reduces channel sounding overhead by more than 50% compared to IEEE 802.11 CSI compression, with a compression ratio of 1/4 providing the best accuracy/feedback-size tradeoff for lowest data latency. Compared to legacy transmissions without MAPC, IEEE 802.11 CSI compression limits Co-BF due to high channel sounding overhead, causing it to underperform the legacy in some situations. However, AE-based CSI compression enables better Co-BF performance with substantial gains in throughput and data latency compared to legacy, demonstrating its promise as an enabler of efficient MAPC operation in future Wi-Fi systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [61] [The Long Delay to Arithmetic Generalization: When Learned Representations Outrun Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13082)
*Laura Gomezjurado Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现Transformer在算法任务中的"顿悟"现象延迟源于解码器瓶颈而非编码器学习不足，通过移植编码器可加速顿悟，数值表示的选择作为归纳偏置显著影响学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Transformer在算法任务训练中出现的"顿悟"现象（长时间延迟后突然泛化）的根本原因，特别是延迟的来源和机制。

Method: 使用编码器-解码器架构研究一步Collatz预测任务，通过因果干预、编码器移植实验、冻结编码器重训练解码器等方法，分析不同数值表示（15种进制）对学习效果的影响。

Result: 编码器在训练早期（几千步）就学会了奇偶性和余数结构，但解码器瓶颈导致准确率长时间停滞；移植训练好的编码器可加速顿悟2.75倍；冻结编码器后重训练解码器可消除平台期，达到97.6%准确率；数值表示的因数分解与Collatz映射对齐时（如24进制）达到99.8%准确率，而二进制完全失败。

Conclusion: 顿悟延迟主要源于解码器瓶颈而非编码器学习不足，数值表示的选择作为归纳偏置通过影响解码器利用局部数字结构的能力，显著控制着同一底层任务的学习难度。

Abstract: Grokking in transformers trained on algorithmic tasks is characterized by a long delay between training-set fit and abrupt generalization, but the source of that delay remains poorly understood. In encoder-decoder arithmetic models, we argue that this delay reflects limited access to already learned structure rather than failure to acquire that structure in the first place. We study one-step Collatz prediction and find that the encoder organizes parity and residue structure within the first few thousand training steps, while output accuracy remains near chance for tens of thousands more. Causal interventions support the decoder bottleneck hypothesis. Transplanting a trained encoder into a fresh model accelerates grokking by 2.75 times, while transplanting a trained decoder actively hurts. Freezing a converged encoder and retraining only the decoder eliminates the plateau entirely and yields 97.6% accuracy, compared to 86.1% for joint training. What makes the decoder's job harder or easier depends on numeral representation. Across 15 bases, those whose factorization aligns with the Collatz map's arithmetic (e.g., base 24) reach 99.8% accuracy, while binary fails completely because its representations collapse and never recover. The choice of base acts as an inductive bias that controls how much local digit structure the decoder can exploit, producing large differences in learnability from the same underlying task.

</details>


### [62] [Adaptive Memory Crystallization for Autonomous AI Agent Learning in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13085)
*Rajat Khanda,Mohammad Baqar Sambuddha Chakrabarti,Satyasaran Changdar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AMC是一种用于持续强化学习的自适应记忆结晶架构，通过模拟记忆从可塑态到稳定态的结晶过程，有效解决灾难性遗忘问题，在多个基准测试中显著提升正向迁移并减少记忆占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主AI智能体在动态环境中面临获取新能力而不遗忘已有知识的持续挑战，即灾难性遗忘问题。现有方法难以在保持旧知识的同时高效整合新经验。

Method: 提出自适应记忆结晶（AMC）架构，受突触标记与捕获理论启发，建立液态-玻璃态-晶态三阶段记忆层次。使用伊藤随机微分方程描述记忆结晶过程，其群体行为由具有闭式Beta稳态分布的福克-普朗克方程刻画。

Result: 理论证明：SDE的适定性和全局收敛性、结晶状态指数收敛性、Q学习误差界和记忆容量下界。实验在Meta-World MT50、Atari 20-game和MuJoCo上显示：正向迁移提升34-43%，灾难性遗忘减少67-80%，内存占用降低62%。

Conclusion: AMC通过形式化记忆结晶过程，为持续强化学习提供了理论严谨且高效的记忆架构，在保持旧知识的同时促进新知识整合，显著优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents operating in dynamic environments face a persistent challenge: acquiring new capabilities without erasing prior knowledge. We present Adaptive Memory Crystallization (AMC), a memory architecture for progressive experience consolidation in continual reinforcement learning.
  AMC is conceptually inspired by the qualitative structure of synaptic tagging and capture (STC) theory, the idea that memories transition through discrete stability phases, but makes no claim to model the underlying molecular or synaptic mechanisms.
  AMC models memory as a continuous crystallization process in which experiences migrate from plastic to stable states according to a multi-objective utility signal. The framework introduces a three-phase memory hierarchy (Liquid--Glass--Crystal) governed by an Itô stochastic differential equation (SDE) whose population-level behavior is captured by an explicit Fokker--Planck equation admitting a closed-form Beta stationary distribution.
  We provide proofs of: (i) well-posedness and global convergence of the crystallization SDE to a unique Beta stationary distribution; (ii) exponential convergence of individual crystallization states to their fixed points, with explicit rates and variance bounds; and (iii) end-to-end Q-learning error bounds and matching memory-capacity lower bounds that link SDE parameters directly to agent performance.
  Empirical evaluation on Meta-World MT50, Atari 20-game sequential learning, and MuJoCo continual locomotion consistently shows improvements in forward transfer (+34--43\% over the strongest baseline), reductions in catastrophic forgetting (67--80\%), and a 62\% decrease in memory footprint.

</details>


### [63] [Design Conditions for Intra-Group Learning of Sequence-Level Rewards: Token Gradient Cancellation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13088)
*Fei Ding,Yongkang Zhang,youwei wang,Zijian Zeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出基于令牌级信用分配的算法设计必要条件：为防止奖励无关漂移，组内目标需保持令牌更新间的梯度可交换性，使弱信用/高频令牌的梯度可抵消。通过最小组内变换恢复或近似共享令牌空间的抵消结构，稳定训练并提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在稀疏终止奖励场景中，组内比较已成为通过强化学习微调推理模型的主导范式。但长期训练常导致无效更新累积（学习税）、解概率漂移和熵崩溃等问题。需要从令牌级信用分配角度建立算法设计的必要条件。

Method: 提出令牌级信用分配视角下的算法设计必要条件：组内目标必须保持梯度在令牌更新间的可交换性，使弱信用/高频令牌的梯度可抵消。识别破坏可交换性的两种常见机制，提出最小组内变换来恢复或近似共享令牌空间的抵消结构。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的变换能稳定训练过程，提高样本效率，并增强最终性能，验证了该设计条件的价值。

Conclusion: 从令牌级信用分配角度提出的算法设计必要条件（梯度可交换性和抵消结构）是解决稀疏终止奖励中组内比较方法长期训练问题的关键。最小组内变换能有效恢复这一结构，提升训练稳定性和模型性能。

Abstract: In sparse termination rewards, intra-group comparisons have become the dominant paradigm for fine-tuning reasoning models via reinforcement learning. However, long-term training often leads to issues like ineffective update accumulation (learning tax), solution probability drift, and entropy collapse. This paper presents a necessary condition for algorithm design from a token-level credit assignment perspective: to prevent reward-irrelevant drift, intra-group objectives must maintain gradient exchangeability across token updates, enabling gradient cancellation on weak-credit/high-frequency tokens. We show that two common mechanisms disrupting exchangeability make "non-cancellation" a structural norm. Based on this, we propose minimal intra-group transformations to restore or approximate the cancellation structure in the shared token space. Experimental results demonstrate that these transformations stabilize training, improve sample efficiency, and enhance final performance, validating the value of this design condition.

</details>


### [64] [Spectral Entropy Collapse as an Empirical Signature of Delayed Generalisation in Grokking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13123)
*Truong Xuan Khanh,Truong Quynh Hoa,Luu Duc Trung,Phan Thanh Duc*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文发现归一化谱熵可作为Transformer模型"顿悟"现象的可预测序参数，揭示了"顿悟"遵循"范数扩张→熵坍缩"的两阶段模式，谱熵阈值能提前预测泛化发生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "顿悟"现象（即模型在长时间记忆后才突然获得泛化能力）缺乏可预测的机制性解释。研究者希望找到能够预测这一转变的标量序参数，从而理解其内在机制。

Method: 使用单层Transformer在群论任务上进行实验，提出归一化谱熵作为序参数。通过因果干预实验（阻止熵坍缩）、范数匹配控制实验，验证熵而非范数驱动转变。建立幂律预测模型，并在阿贝尔群和非阿贝尔群上验证机制普适性。

Result: 发现：(1)顿悟遵循两阶段模式：范数扩张后熵坍缩；(2)归一化谱熵在100%实验中稳定跨越阈值~0.61后才泛化；(3)阻止熵坍缩延迟顿悟5020步；(4)幂律模型能预测顿悟起始时间，误差4.1%；(5)机制在阿贝尔群和非阿贝尔群均成立。MLP有熵坍缩但无顿悟，证明熵坍缩必要但不充分。

Conclusion: 归一化谱熵是顿悟现象的可预测序参数，揭示了"范数扩张→熵坍缩"的两阶段机制。该机制在Transformer架构中普适，但架构本身对顿悟发生至关重要（MLP虽有熵坍缩却无顿悟）。这为理解深度学习中的延迟泛化提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Grokking -- delayed generalisation long after memorisation -- lacks a predictive mechanistic explanation. We identify the normalised spectral entropy $\tilde{H}(t)$ of the representation covariance as a scalar order parameter for this transition, validated on 1-layer Transformers on group-theoretic tasks. Five contributions: (i) Grokking follows a two-phase pattern: norm expansion then entropy collapse. (ii) $\tilde{H}$ crosses a stable threshold $\tilde{H}^* \approx 0.61$ before generalisation in 100% of runs (mean lead: 1,020 steps). (iii) A causal intervention preventing collapse delays grokking by +5,020 steps ($p=0.044$); a norm-matched control ($n=30$, $p=5\times10^{-5}$) confirms entropy -- not norm -- drives the transition. (iv) A power-law $ΔT = C_1(\tilde{H}-\tilde{H}^*)^γ+C_2$ ($R^2=0.543$) predicts grokking onset with 4.1% error. (v) The mechanism holds across abelian ($\mathbb{Z}/97\mathbb{Z}$) and non-abelian ($S_5$) groups. Crucially, MLPs show entropy collapse without grokking, proving collapse is necessary but not sufficient -- architecture matters. Code: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/grokking-entropy

</details>


### [65] [Analog Optical Inference on Million-Record Mortgage Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13251)
*Sofia Berloff,Pavel Koptev,Konstantin Malkov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究首次将模拟光学计算机应用于大规模现实世界分类任务（抵押贷款审批），识别了三种精度损失来源：编码限制、架构限制和硬件保真度，发现硬件非理想性对精度无显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟光学计算机在机器学习推理中具有高效能潜力，但先前研究仅限于小规模图像基准测试。本研究旨在评估其在现实世界大规模分类任务（抵押贷款审批）中的性能，并系统分析精度损失的来源。

Method: 使用美国HMDA数据库的584万条抵押贷款记录，构建模拟光学计算机的数字孪生模型进行抵押贷款审批分类。对比分析XGBoost等数字模型，通过控制变量法分离三种精度损失来源：1）编码限制（127位二进制编码）；2）架构限制（光学核心宽度从16通道扩展到48通道）；3）硬件非理想性（七种校准的硬件非理想性）。

Result: 在原始19个特征上，模拟光学计算机达到94.6%平衡精度（5126个参数，其中1024个光学参数），XGBoost为97.9%，存在3.3个百分点的差距。光学核心宽度从16通道扩展到48通道仅缩小0.5个百分点差距，表明主要是架构限制而非硬件限制。采用127位二进制编码后，所有模型精度降至89.4-89.6%，编码成本对数字模型为8个百分点，对模拟光学计算机为5个百分点。七种校准的硬件非理想性未造成可测量的精度损失。

Conclusion: 研究识别了模拟光学计算机精度损失的三个层次：编码限制、架构限制和硬件保真度。硬件非理想性不是当前主要瓶颈，未来改进应聚焦于编码方案和架构优化。这是首次将模拟光学计算机应用于大规模现实世界分类任务，为后续研究提供了明确的改进方向。

Abstract: Analog optical computers promise large efficiency gains for machine learning inference, yet no demonstration has moved beyond small-scale image benchmarks. We benchmark the analog optical computer (AOC) digital twin on mortgage approval classification from 5.84 million U.S. HMDA records and separate three sources of accuracy loss. On the original 19 features, the AOC reaches 94.6% balanced accuracy with 5,126 parameters (1,024 optical), compared with 97.9% for XGBoost; the 3.3 percentage-point gap narrows by only 0.5pp when the optical core is widened from 16 to 48 channels, suggesting an architectural rather than hardware limitation. Restricting all models to a shared 127-bit binary encoding drops every model to 89.4--89.6%, with an encoding cost of 8pp for digital models and 5pp for the AOC. Seven calibrated hardware non-idealities impose no measurable penalty. The three resulting layers of limitation (encoding, architecture, hardware fidelity) locate where accuracy is lost and what to improve next.

</details>


### [66] [SFT-GRPO Data Overlap as a Post-Training Hyperparameter for Autoformalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13515)
*Xiaole Su,Kasey Zhang,Andy Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究通过控制实验发现，在SFT后接GRPO的微调流程中，保持两个阶段训练数据不重叠（0%重叠）能显著提升模型性能，而完全重叠（100%重叠）则使GRPO阶段几乎无效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探究监督微调（SFT）与组相对策略优化（GRPO）两个阶段训练数据重叠程度对最终模型性能的影响。目前SFT-GRPO是常见的后训练流程，但数据重叠这一超参数尚未得到系统研究。

Method: 采用控制变量法，在Qwen3-8B模型上进行Lean 4自动形式化后训练，设置六种不同训练配置：基础模型、仅SFT、仅GRPO，以及三种SFT+GRPO配置（0%、30%、100%数据重叠）。保持SFT和GRPO数据总量不变，仅改变重叠比例。评估使用Gaokao-Formal和PutnamBench数据集，采用编译通过率（compile pass@k）和语义正确率（semantic pass@k）双重指标。

Result: 结果显示：1）数据重叠程度与性能呈单调负相关，重叠越低性能越好；2）0%重叠时，GRPO在Gaokao上带来10.4个百分点的语义准确率提升；3）100%重叠时，GRPO阶段几乎无效，性能与仅SFT相当；4）双重评估揭示编译与语义准确率差距可达30个百分点以上，仅用编译指标会掩盖这一差距。

Conclusion: 这是首个对SFT-GRPO数据重叠作为后训练超参数的系统研究，证明保持两阶段训练数据不重叠能显著提升模型性能且不增加计算成本。研究强调了双重评估的重要性，仅依赖编译指标会严重高估模型实际能力。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) followed by Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is a common post-training recipe. We conduct a controlled ablation over SFT-GRPO data overlap, evaluating Qwen3-8B (thinking disabled) post-trained for Lean 4 autoformalization under six conditions that differ solely in training recipe: a base model, SFT-only, GRPO-only, and three SFT+GRPO configurations where 0 percent, 30 percent, or 100 percent of the GRPO prompts coincide with the SFT corpus. Keeping SFT and GRPO data disjoint consistently outperforms full overlap at zero additional compute cost. Evaluating on Gaokao-Formal and PutnamBench under both compile pass at k and semantic pass at k assessed by an LLM judge, we find that lower overlap is monotonically associated with higher compilation and semantic accuracy. At 0 percent overlap, GRPO yields a 10.4 percentage point semantic gain over SFT alone on Gaokao, while at 100 percent overlap both metrics remain flat, rendering the GRPO stage effectively redundant. We further show that dual-metric evaluation reveals compile semantic gaps exceeding 30 percentage points for the highest compiling models, a disparity invisible under compile-only benchmarking. To our knowledge, this is the first controlled investigation of SFT-GRPO data overlap as a post-training hyperparameter, demonstrating how model behavior varies based on the degree of data sharing between training stages.

</details>


### [67] [Parameter-efficient Quantum Multi-task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13560)
*Hevish Cowlessur,Chandra Thapa,Tansu Alpcan,Seyit Camtepe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出参数高效的量子多任务学习框架，用全量子预测头替代传统任务特定线性头，在保持性能的同时显著减少参数数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务学习中任务特定参数随任务数量快速增长，传统硬参数共享架构面临参数效率挑战。量子机器学习中变分量子电路能在有限参数预算下提供高维希尔伯特空间中的表达性表示，为设计参数高效的多任务头提供了新途径。

Method: 提出混合架构的量子多任务学习框架：共享任务无关的量子编码阶段，后接轻量级任务特定的ansatz块。在共享表示维度随任务数增长的控制条件下，量子头的参数成本呈线性增长，而经典头呈二次增长。

Result: 在自然语言处理、医学影像和多模态讽刺检测三个多任务基准测试中，性能与经典硬参数共享基线相当或更好，同时以显著更少的头参数持续优于现有混合量子多任务学习模型。在噪声模拟器和真实量子硬件上验证了可行性。

Conclusion: 量子多任务学习框架通过全量子预测头实现了参数效率，在保持任务专业化的同时减少了参数增长，为量子机器学习在多任务场景中的应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Multi-task learning (MTL) improves generalization and data efficiency by jointly learning related tasks through shared representations. In the widely used hard-parameter-sharing setting, a shared backbone is combined with task-specific prediction heads. However, task-specific parameters can grow rapidly with the number of tasks. Therefore, designing multi-task heads that preserve task specialization while improving parameter efficiency remains a key challenge. In Quantum Machine Learning (QML), variational quantum circuits (VQCs) provide a compact mechanism for mapping classical data to quantum states residing in high-dimensional Hilbert spaces, enabling expressive representations within constrained parameter budgets. We propose a parameter-efficient quantum multi-task learning (QMTL) framework that replaces conventional task-specific linear heads with a fully quantum prediction head in a hybrid architecture. The model consists of a VQC with a shared, task-independent quantum encoding stage, followed by lightweight task-specific ansatz blocks enabling localized task adaptation while maintaining compact parameterization. Under a controlled and capacity-matched formulation where the shared representation dimension grows with the number of tasks, our parameter-scaling analysis demonstrates that a standard classical head exhibits quadratic growth, whereas the proposed quantum head parameter cost scales linearly. We evaluate QMTL on three multi-task benchmarks spanning natural language processing, medical imaging, and multimodal sarcasm detection, where we achieve performance comparable to, and in some cases exceeding, classical hard-parameter-sharing baselines while consistently outperforming existing hybrid quantum MTL models with substantially fewer head parameters. We further demonstrate QMTL's executability on noisy simulators and real quantum hardware, illustrating its feasibility.

</details>


### [68] [When Reasoning Models Hurt Behavioral Simulation: A Solver-Sampler Mismatch in Multi-Agent LLM Negotiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11840)
*Sandro Andric*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出在行为模拟中，增强推理能力的大型语言模型可能成为更好的"求解器"但更差的"模拟器"，因为会过度优化策略主导行为，破坏妥协导向的终端行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型语言模型越来越多地用于社会、经济和政策模拟中，普遍假设更强的推理能力应该提高模拟保真度。但作者认为当目标是采样合理的有限理性行为而非解决策略问题时，这一假设可能失效。

Method: 在三个多智能体谈判环境中研究求解器-采样器不匹配问题：模糊分散权威交易限制场景、模糊统一反对交易限制场景、以及新的紧急电力管理电网削减案例。比较三种反思条件（无反思、有限反思、原生推理），在两个主要模型家族中进行，并扩展到GPT-4.1和GPT-5.2的直接运行。
