Conclusion: 提出的RLVR-PET方法通过精细的探索-利用权衡机制，成功解决了RLVR训练中的关键挑战，为LLM推理能力的进一步提升提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has catalyzed significant advances in the reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs). However, effectively managing the exploration and exploitation trade-off remains a critical challenge. In this paper, we fully analyze the exploration and exploitation dilemma of extremely hard and easy samples during the training and propose a new fine-grained trade-off mechanism. Concretely, we introduce a perplexity space disentangling strategy that divides the sample space into distinct exploration (high perplexity) and exploitation (low perplexity) subspaces, thereby mining fine-grained samples requiring exploration-exploitation trade-off. Subsequently, we propose a bidirectional reward allocation mechanism with a minimum impact on verification rewards to implement perplexity-guided exploration and exploitation, enabling more stable policy optimization. Finally, we have evaluated our method on two mainstream tasks: mathematical reasoning and function calling, and experimental results demonstrate the superiority of the proposed method, confirming its effectiveness in enhancing LLM performance by fine-grained exploration-exploitation trade-off.

</details>


### [109] [ASTER: Latent Pseudo-Anomaly Generation for Unsupervised Time-Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13924)
*Romain Hermary,Samet Hicsonmez,Dan Pineau,Abd El Rahman Shabayek,Djamila Aouada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ASTER：一种通过潜在空间生成伪异常来训练Transformer异常分类器的无监督时间序列异常检测框架，利用预训练LLM增强时空表示


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列异常检测面临标注数据稀缺、异常罕见且异质性的挑战。现有无监督方法依赖重构或预测，难以处理复杂数据；基于嵌入的方法需要领域特定的异常合成和固定距离度量。

Method: 提出ASTER框架：1）在潜在空间中直接生成伪异常，避免手工异常注入和领域专业知识需求；2）使用潜在空间解码器生成定制化伪异常训练Transformer异常分类器；3）利用预训练LLM丰富该空间的时空上下文表示。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的实验表明，ASTER实现了最先进的性能，为基于LLM的时间序列异常检测设立了新标准。

Conclusion: ASTER通过潜在空间伪异常生成和LLM增强表示，有效解决了时间序列异常检测中标注数据稀缺和异常异质性的问题，为无监督异常检测提供了新方向。

Abstract: Time-series anomaly detection (TSAD) is critical in domains such as industrial monitoring, healthcare, and cybersecurity, but it remains challenging due to rare and heterogeneous anomalies and the scarcity of labelled data. This scarcity makes unsupervised approaches predominant, yet existing methods often rely on reconstruction or forecasting, which struggle with complex data, or on embedding-based approaches that require domain-specific anomaly synthesis and fixed distance metrics. We propose ASTER, a framework that generates pseudo-anomalies directly in the latent space, avoiding handcrafted anomaly injections and the need for domain expertise. A latent-space decoder produces tailored pseudo-anomalies to train a Transformer-based anomaly classifier, while a pre-trained LLM enriches the temporal and contextual representations of this space. Experiments on three benchmark datasets show that ASTER achieves state-of-the-art performance and sets a new standard for LLM-based TSAD.

</details>


### [110] [Unsupervised Anomaly Detection in Process-Complex Industrial Time Series: A Real-World Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13928)
*Sergej Krasnikov,Lukas Meitz,Samineh Bagheri,Michael Heider,Thorsten Schöler,Jörg Hähner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文对工业时间序列数据的异常检测进行了实证研究，发现工业数据比基准数据集更复杂，传统方法如隔离森林效果不佳，而时间卷积自编码器在捕捉工业过程的多尺度动态方面表现最稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业时间序列数据由于来自多阶段、异质性的生产过程，其复杂度远高于常用的基准数据集。在简化条件下验证的异常检测方法往往无法推广到实际工业环境中，因此需要研究能够处理这种复杂性的模型。

Method: 使用从实际运行的工业机械收集的独特数据集进行实证研究。从经典的隔离森林基线开始，扩展到多种自编码器架构进行评估，包括时间卷积自编码器、循环自编码器和变分自编码器。

Result: 隔离森林无法有效建模数据中的非周期性、多尺度动态。自编码器整体表现更好，其中时间卷积自编码器获得最稳健的性能，而循环和变分自编码器需要更仔细的调参。

Conclusion: 工业时间序列数据的复杂性需要专门设计的异常检测方法。时间卷积自编码器在捕捉工业过程引起的变异性方面表现最佳，为实际工业应用提供了有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Industrial time-series data from real production environments exhibits substantially higher complexity than commonly used benchmark datasets, primarily due to heterogeneous, multi-stage operational processes. As a result, anomaly detection methods validated under simplified conditions often fail to generalize to industrial settings. This work presents an empirical study on a unique dataset collected from fully operational industrial machinery, explicitly capturing pronounced process-induced variability.
  We evaluate which model classes are capable of capturing this complexity, starting with a classical Isolation Forest baseline and extending to multiple autoencoder architectures. Experimental results show that Isolation Forest is insufficient for modeling the non-periodic, multi-scale dynamics present in the data, whereas autoencoders consistently perform better. Among them, temporal convolutional autoencoders achieve the most robust performance, while recurrent and variational variants require more careful tuning.

</details>


### [111] [HINTBench: Horizon-agent Intrinsic Non-attack Trajectory Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13954)
*Jiacheng Wang,Jinchang Hou,Fabian Wang,Ping Jian,Chenfu Bao,Zhonghou Lv*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出内在风险审计概念，建立HINTBench基准评估智能体在非攻击条件下的内在风险，发现现有模型在风险步骤定位和故障诊断方面存在显著能力差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体安全评估主要关注外部诱导风险，但智能体在良性条件下仍可能进入不安全轨迹。本文研究这种互补但未被充分探索的内在风险设置，其中内在故障保持潜伏状态，在长期执行中传播，最终导致严重后果。

Method: 引入非攻击内在风险审计方法，提出HINTBench基准，包含629个智能体轨迹（523个有风险，106个安全；平均33步），支持三个任务：风险检测、风险步骤定位和内在故障类型识别。标注基于统一的五约束分类法。

Result: 实验显示显著能力差距：强LLM在轨迹级风险检测上表现良好，但在风险步骤定位上的性能降至低于35 Strict-F1，细粒度故障诊断更为困难。现有防护模型在此设置下迁移效果差。

Conclusion: 这些发现确立了内在风险审计作为智能体安全的一个开放挑战，需要新的方法来检测和缓解智能体在非攻击条件下的内在风险。

Abstract: Existing agent-safety evaluation has focused mainly on externally induced risks. Yet agents may still enter unsafe trajectories under benign conditions. We study this complementary but underexplored setting through the lens of \emph{intrinsic} risk, where intrinsic failures remain latent, propagate across long-horizon execution, and eventually lead to high-consequence outcomes. To evaluate this setting, we introduce \emph{non-attack intrinsic risk auditing} and present \textbf{HINTBench}, a benchmark of 629 agent trajectories (523 risky, 106 safe; 33 steps on average) supporting three tasks: risk detection, risk-step localization, and intrinsic failure-type identification. Its annotations are organized under a unified five-constraint taxonomy. Experiments reveal a substantial capability gap: strong LLMs perform well on trajectory-level risk detection, but their performance drops to below 35 Strict-F1 on risk-step localization, while fine-grained failure diagnosis proves even harder. Existing guard models transfer poorly to this setting. These findings establish intrinsic risk auditing as an open challenge for agent safety.

</details>


### [112] [PRiMeFlow: Capturing Complex Expression Heterogeneity in Perturbation Response Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13986)
*Zichao Yan,Yan Wu,Mica Xu Ji,Chaitra Agrahar,Esther Wershof,Marcel Nassar,Mehrshad Sadria,Ridvan Eksi,Vladimir Trifonov,Ignacio Ibarra,Telmo Felgueira,Błażej Osiński,Rory Stark*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PRiMeFlow是一种基于流匹配的端到端方法，直接在基因表达空间中建模遗传和小分子扰动效应，能够准确近似单细胞基因表达的经验分布，并在PerturBench基准测试中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在计算机中预测细胞状态的扰动效应可以大规模识别细胞行为的驱动因素并加速药物发现。然而，由于单细胞基因表达固有的异质性和复杂的潜在基因依赖性，建模仍然面临挑战。

Method: PRiMeFlow采用端到端的流匹配方法，直接在基因表达空间中建模遗传和小分子扰动效应。该方法使用分布拟合方法近似单细胞基因表达的经验分布，并通过消融研究验证了关键设计选择，如在基因表达空间操作和使用U-Net架构参数化速度场。

Result: PRiMeFlow在PerturBench基准测试中表现出色，能够准确近似单细胞基因表达的经验分布。基于PRiMeFlow架构的模型在首届ARC虚拟细胞挑战赛中获得了通用奖。

Conclusion: PRiMeFlow是一种有效的流匹配方法，能够直接建模基因表达空间中的扰动效应，为大规模识别细胞行为驱动因素和加速药物发现提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Predicting the effects of perturbations in-silico on cell state can identify drivers of cell behavior at scale and accelerate drug discovery. However, modeling challenges remain due to the inherent heterogeneity of single cell gene expression and the complex, latent gene dependencies. Here, we present PRiMeFlow, an end-to-end flow matching based approach to directly model the effects of genetic and small molecule perturbations in the gene expression space. The distribution-fitting approach taken by PRiMeFlow enables it to accurately approximate the empirical distribution of single-cell gene expression, which we demonstrate through extensive benchmarking inside PerturBench. Through ablation studies, we also validate important model design choices such as operating in gene expression space and parameterizing the velocity field with a U-Net architecture. The PRiMeFlow architecture was used as the basis for the model that won the Generalist Prize in the first ARC Virtual Cell Challenge.

</details>


### [113] [Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Methane Sorption: Cross-Gas Transfer Learning, Ensemble Collapse Under Physics Constraints, and Monte Carlo Dropout Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13992)
*Mohammad Nooraiepour,Zezhang Song,Wei Li,Sarah Perez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种物理信息迁移学习框架，通过弹性权重巩固、煤质特征工程和三阶段课程学习，将氢吸附PINN迁移到甲烷吸附预测，在993个煤样数据上实现R²=0.932，比传统等温线提升227%，并评估了五种贝叶斯不确定性量化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有甲烷吸附预测模型缺乏将热力学一致性、跨地质系统知识迁移效率和校准不确定性估计相结合的能力，特别是在数据稀缺的异质煤阶系统中。

Method: 采用物理信息迁移学习框架：1) 通过弹性权重巩固将氢吸附PINN迁移到甲烷吸附预测；2) 煤质特征工程；3) 三阶段课程学习平衡迁移保持与热力学微调；4) 评估五种贝叶斯不确定性量化方法（蒙特卡洛Dropout、深度集成等）。

Result: 在114个独立煤样实验的993个平衡测量数据上，框架达到R²=0.932，比仅基于压力的经典等温线提升227%。氢预训练比随机初始化降低18.9%RMSE并加快19.4%收敛。蒙特卡洛Dropout在物理约束架构中表现最佳，而深度集成因共享物理约束导致性能下降。SHAP和ALE分析确认学习表征具有物理可解释性。

Conclusion: 蒙特卡洛Dropout是该物理约束迁移学习框架中最佳的不确定性量化方法，跨气体迁移学习是地质材料建模的数据高效策略，学习表征与煤吸附机制物理一致。

Abstract: Accurate methane sorption prediction across heterogeneous coal ranks requires models that combine thermodynamic consistency, efficient knowledge transfer across data-scarce geological systems, and calibrated uncertainty estimates, capabilities that are rarely addressed together in existing frameworks. We present a physics-informed transfer learning framework that adapts a hydrogen sorption PINN to methane sorption prediction via Elastic Weight Consolidation, coal-specific feature engineering, and a three-phase curriculum that progressively balances transfer preservation with thermodynamic fine-tuning. Trained on 993 equilibrium measurements from 114 independent coal experiments spanning lignite to anthracite, the framework achieves R2 = 0.932 on held-out coal samples, a 227% improvement over pressure-only classical isotherms, while hydrogen pre-training delivers 18.9% lower RMSE and 19.4% faster convergence than random initialization. Five Bayesian uncertainty quantification approaches reveal a systematic divergence in performance across physics-constrained architectures. Monte Carlo Dropout achieves well-calibrated uncertainty at minimal overhead, while deep ensembles, regardless of architectural diversity or initialization strategy, exhibit performance degradation because shared physics constraints narrow the admissible solution manifold. SHAP and ALE analyses confirm that learned representations remain physically interpretable and aligned with established coal sorption mechanisms: moisture-volatile interactions are most influential, pressure-temperature coupling captures thermodynamic co-dependence, and features exhibit non-monotonic effects. These results identify Monte Carlo Dropout as the best-performing UQ method in this physics-constrained transfer learning framework, and demonstrate cross-gas transfer learning as a data-efficient strategy for geological material modeling.

</details>


### [114] [Parameter Importance is Not Static: Evolving Parameter Isolation for Supervised Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14010)
*Zekai Lin,Chao Xue,Di Liang,Xingsheng Han,Peiyang Liu,Xianjie Wu,Lei Jiang,Yu Lu,Haibo Shi,Shuang Liang,Minlong Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EPI提出动态参数隔离框架，通过在线评估参数重要性并周期性更新隔离掩码，解决SFT中的任务干扰和灾难性遗忘问题，相比静态隔离方法效果更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数隔离方法假设参数重要性是静态的，但实际训练中参数重要性会随时间漂移，静态隔离无法适应这种动态变化，导致次优性能。

Method: 提出Evolving Parameter Isolation (EPI)框架，基于梯度信号在线评估参数重要性，周期性更新隔离掩码，动态保护新出现的任务关键参数，同时释放过时参数以恢复模型可塑性。

Result: 在多任务基准测试中，EPI相比静态隔离和标准微调方法，能更有效地减少任务干扰和灾难性遗忘，同时提升整体泛化能力。

Conclusion: 参数重要性在训练过程中会发生动态变化，隔离机制需要与学习动态同步演化，EPI框架通过动态参数隔离有效解决了SFT中的任务干扰和遗忘问题。

Abstract: Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) of large language models often suffers from task interference and catastrophic forgetting. Recent approaches alleviate this issue by isolating task-critical parameters during training. However, these methods represent a static solution to a dynamic problem, assuming that parameter importance remains fixed once identified. In this work, we empirically demonstrate that parameter importance exhibits temporal drift over the course of training. To address this, we propose Evolving Parameter Isolation (EPI), a fine-tuning framework that adapts isolation decisions based on online estimates of parameter importance. Instead of freezing a fixed subset of parameters, EPI periodically updates isolation masks using gradient-based signals, enabling the model to protect emerging task-critical parameters while releasing outdated ones to recover plasticity. Experiments on diverse multi-task benchmarks demonstrate that EPI consistently reduces interference and forgetting compared to static isolation and standard fine-tuning, while improving overall generalization. Our analysis highlights the necessity of synchronizing isolation mechanisms with the evolving dynamics of learning diverse abilities.

</details>


### [115] [MAny: Merge Anything for Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14016)
*Zijian Gao,Wangwang Jia,Xingxing Zhang,Pengfei Qian,Tao Sun,Bo Ding,Yong Dou,Huaimin Wang,Kele Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MAny框架通过跨模态投影合并和低秩参数合并解决多模态持续指令调优中的双重遗忘问题，无需额外训练即可实现任务知识融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态持续指令调优面临灾难性遗忘问题，现有研究主要关注推理语言主干，但忽视了跨模态投影空间中的感知漂移和低秩参数空间中的推理崩溃这一双重遗忘现象。

Method: 提出MAny框架，包含跨模态投影合并和低秩参数合并两个核心组件。CPM通过视觉原型指导自适应合并跨模态视觉表示，恢复感知对齐；LPM通过递归最小二乘法递归合并低秩权重矩阵，消除任务特定模块间的相互干扰，提供最优融合轨迹的闭式解。

Result: 在UCIT基准测试中，MAny在两个不同的MLLM上分别实现了比最先进方法高出8.57%和2.85%的最终平均准确率优势，表现出卓越的性能和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MAny框架有效解决了多模态持续指令调优中的双重遗忘问题，通过无训练的代数操作实现任务知识融合，为MLLM的顺序任务适应提供了高效可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning (MCIT) is essential for sequential task adaptation of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) but is severely restricted by catastrophic forgetting. While existing literature focuses on the reasoning language backbone, in this work, we expose a critical yet neglected dual-forgetting phenomenon across both perception drift in Cross-modal Projection Space and reasoning collapse in Low-rank Parameter Space. To resolve this, we present \textbf{MAny} (\textbf{M}erge \textbf{Any}thing), a framework that merges task-specific knowledge through \textbf{C}ross-modal \textbf{P}rojection \textbf{M}erging (\textbf{CPM}) and \textbf{L}ow-rank \textbf{P}arameter \textbf{M}erging (\textbf{LPM}). Specifically, CPM recovers perceptual alignment by adaptively merging cross-modal visual representations via visual-prototype guidance, ensuring accurate feature recovery during inference. Simultaneously, LPM eliminates mutual interference among task-specific low-rank modules by recursively merging low-rank weight matrices. By leveraging recursive least squares, LPM provides a closed-form solution that mathematically guarantees an optimal fusion trajectory for reasoning stability. Notably, MAny operates as a training-free paradigm that achieves knowledge merging via efficient CPU-based algebraic operations, eliminating additional gradient-based optimization beyond initial tuning. Our extensive evaluations confirm the superior performance and robustness of MAny across multiple MLLMs and benchmarks. Specifically, on the UCIT benchmark, MAny achieves significant leads of up to 8.57\% and 2.85\% in final average accuracy over state-of-the-art methods across two different MLLMs, respectively.

</details>


### [116] [Momentum Further Constrains Sharpness at the Edge of Stochastic Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14108)
*Arseniy Andreyev,Advikar Ananthkumar,Marc Walden,Tomaso Poggio,Pierfrancesco Beneventano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SGD with momentum exhibits batch-size-dependent stability regimes: 小批量时动量放大随机波动，偏好平坦区域；大批量时动量恢复经典稳定作用，偏好尖锐区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动量和小批量梯度在深度学习优化中广泛应用，但尚不清楚它们是否在类似的稳定性边界区域运行。需要探究动量SGD在不同批量大小下的稳定性行为及其对优化解的影响。

Method: 通过分析SGD with momentum的稳定性边界，研究批量大小对稳定性的影响。引入Batch Sharpness（期望方向性小批量曲率）概念，分析其在两个不同批量大小区域的行为。

Result: 发现动量SGD存在类似随机稳定性边界（EoSS-like）的机制，其行为依赖于批量大小：小批量时Batch Sharpness收敛到较低平台2(1-β)/η，动量放大随机波动，偏好平坦区域；大批量时收敛到较高平台2(1+β)/η，动量恢复经典稳定作用，偏好尖锐区域。

Conclusion: 动量SGD的稳定性行为具有批量大小依赖性，这不同于单一动量调整稳定性阈值的解释。这一发现对超参数调优和动量与批量大小的耦合具有重要启示。

Abstract: Recent work suggests that (stochastic) gradient descent self-organizes near an instability boundary, shaping both optimization and the solutions found. Momentum and mini-batch gradients are widely used in practical deep learning optimization, but it remains unclear whether they operate in a comparable regime of instability. We demonstrate that SGD with momentum exhibits an Edge of Stochastic Stability (EoSS)-like regime with batch-size-dependent behavior that cannot be explained by a single momentum-adjusted stability threshold. Batch Sharpness (the expected directional mini-batch curvature) stabilizes in two distinct regimes: at small batch sizes it converges to a lower plateau $2(1-β)/η$, reflecting amplification of stochastic fluctuations by momentum and favoring flatter regions than vanilla SGD; at large batch sizes it converges to a higher plateau $2(1+β)/η$, where momentum recovers its classical stabilizing effect and favors sharper regions consistent with full-batch dynamics. We further show that this aligns with linear stability thresholds and discuss the implications for hyperparameter tuning and coupling.

</details>


### [117] [Complex Interpolation of Matrices with an application to Multi-Manifold Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14118)
*Adi Arbel,Stefan Steinerberger,Ronen Talmon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究两个对称正定矩阵A和B的插值A^{1-x}B^x的谱性质，探讨矩阵间"共同结构"的存在性及其在多流形学习中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究对称正定矩阵插值的谱性质，以理解矩阵间共享特征结构的存在性，为多视图数据中的共同和独特潜在结构识别提供理论基础。

Method: 分析插值矩阵A^{1-x}B^x的谱性质，研究算子范数\|A^{1-x}B^x\|的对数线性性质与原始矩阵共享特征向量之间的关系，建立稳定性界限。

Result: 算子范数的精确对数线性等价于原始矩阵存在共享特征向量；近似对数线性强制主奇异向量与两个矩阵的主导特征向量对齐。

Conclusion: 这些结果为多流形学习框架提供了理论基础，能够识别多视图数据中的共同和独特潜在结构。

Abstract: Given two symmetric positive-definite matrices $A, B \in \mathbb{R}^{n \times n}$, we study the spectral properties of the interpolation $A^{1-x} B^x$ for $0 \leq x \leq 1$. The presence of `common structures' in $A$ and $B$, eigenvectors pointing in a similar direction, can be investigated using this interpolation perspective. Generically, exact log-linearity of the operator norm $\|A^{1-x} B^x\|$ is equivalent to the existence of a shared eigenvector in the original matrices; stability bounds show that approximate log-linearity forces principal singular vectors to align with leading eigenvectors of both matrices. These results give rise to and provide theoretical justification for a multi-manifold learning framework that identifies common and distinct latent structures in multiview data.

</details>


### [118] [LongCoT: Benchmarking Long-Horizon Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14140)
*Sumeet Ramesh Motwani,Daniel Nichols,Charles London,Peggy Li,Fabio Pizzati,Acer Blake,Hasan Hammoud,Tavish McDonald,Akshat Naik,Alesia Ivanova,Vignesh Baskaran,Ivan Laptev,Ruben Glatt,Tal Ben-Nun,Philip Torr,Natasha Jaques,Ameya Prabhu,Brian Bartoldson,Bhavya Kailkhura,Christian Schroeder de Witt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LongCoT是一个包含2500个专家设计问题的基准测试，用于直接测量前沿模型在长链条思维推理方面的能力，目前最佳模型准确率低于10%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语言模型被部署用于复杂自主任务，其在长时程上的准确推理能力变得至关重要。需要直接测量模型在长链条思维推理方面的能力，现有基准无法充分评估这一关键能力。

Method: 引入LongCoT基准，包含2500个专家设计的问题，涵盖化学、数学、计算机科学、国际象棋和逻辑等领域。每个问题由简短输入和可验证答案组成，解决需要导航包含数十万推理token的相互依赖步骤图。每个局部步骤对前沿模型都是可处理的，因此失败反映了长时程推理的局限性。

Result: 发布时最佳模型在LongCoT上的准确率低于10%（GPT 5.2：9.8%；Gemini 3 Pro：6.1%），揭示了当前能力在长时程推理方面存在显著差距。

Conclusion: LongCoT为长时程推理提供了严格的测量标准，能够追踪前沿模型在扩展时间范围内可靠推理的能力，揭示了当前模型在这一关键能力上的重要局限性。

Abstract: As language models are increasingly deployed for complex autonomous tasks, their ability to reason accurately over longer horizons becomes critical. An essential component of this ability is planning and managing a long, complex chain-of-thought (CoT). We introduce LongCoT, a scalable benchmark of 2,500 expert-designed problems spanning chemistry, mathematics, computer science, chess, and logic to isolate and directly measure the long-horizon CoT reasoning capabilities of frontier models. Problems consist of a short input with a verifiable answer; solving them requires navigating a graph of interdependent steps that span tens to hundreds of thousands of reasoning tokens. Each local step is individually tractable for frontier models, so failures reflect long-horizon reasoning limitations. At release, the best models achieve <10% accuracy (GPT 5.2: 9.8%; Gemini 3 Pro: 6.1%) on LongCoT, revealing a substantial gap in current capabilities. Overall, LongCoT provides a rigorous measure of long-horizon reasoning, tracking the ability of frontier models to reason reliably over extended periods.

</details>


### [119] [From $P(y|x)$ to $P(y)$: Investigating Reinforcement Learning in Pre-train Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14142)
*Yuqiao Tan,Minzheng Wang,Bo Liu,Zichen Liu,Tian Liang,Shizhu He,Jun Zhao,Kang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PreRL方法，直接在预训练空间优化边际分布P(y)以突破传统RLVR的局限性，并发现负样本强化(NSR)机制能有效驱动推理，最终提出双空间强化学习(DSRL)策略，通过预训练空间剪枝和标准RL微调相结合，显著提升LLM推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于可验证奖励的强化学习(RLVR)虽然能优化条件分布P(y|x)，但其潜力受限于基础模型的现有输出分布。优化预训练空间的边际分布P(y)能解决这一瓶颈，但传统预训练依赖静态语料库进行被动学习，导致分布偏移，阻碍了针对性的推理增强。

Method: 提出PreRL方法，直接在预训练空间应用奖励驱动的在线更新来优化P(y)。理论证明log P(y)和log P(y|x)之间的梯度对齐，使PreRL成为标准RL的可行替代。发现负样本强化(NSR)机制能有效驱动推理，通过快速剪枝错误推理空间并激发内源性反思行为。最终提出双空间强化学习(DSRL)策略，采用策略重生方法：先用NSR-PreRL初始化模型以扩展推理视野，再过渡到标准RL进行细粒度优化。

Result: NSR-PreRL显著增加了转换思维和反思思维，分别提升了14.89倍和6.54倍。DSRL在广泛实验中持续优于强基线方法，证明预训练空间剪枝能有效引导策略进入精炼的正确推理子空间。

Conclusion: PreRL通过优化预训练空间的边际分布P(y)突破了传统RLVR的局限性，NSR机制是推理的有效驱动力，而DSRL策略通过预训练空间剪枝和标准RL微调相结合，为LLM推理能力提升提供了有效框架。

Abstract: While reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) significantly enhances LLM reasoning by optimizing the conditional distribution P(y|x), its potential is fundamentally bounded by the base model's existing output distribution. Optimizing the marginal distribution P(y) in the Pre-train Space addresses this bottleneck by encoding reasoning ability and preserving broad exploration capacity. Yet, conventional pre-training relies on static corpora for passive learning, leading to a distribution shift that hinders targeted reasoning enhancement. In this paper, we introduce PreRL (Pre-train Space RL), which applies reward-driven online updates directly to P(y). We theoretically and empirically validate the strong gradient alignment between log P(y) and log P(y|x), establishing PreRL as a viable surrogate for standard RL. Furthermore, we uncover a critical mechanism: Negative Sample Reinforcement (NSR) within PreRL serves as an exceptionally effective driver for reasoning. NSR-PreRL rapidly prunes incorrect reasoning spaces while stimulating endogenous reflective behaviors, increasing transition and reflection thoughts by 14.89x and 6.54x, respectively. Leveraging these insights, we propose Dual Space RL (DSRL), a Policy Reincarnation strategy that initializes models with NSR-PreRL to expand the reasoning horizon before transitioning to standard RL for fine-grained optimization. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DSRL consistently outperforms strong baselines, proving that pre-train space pruning effectively steers the policy toward a refined correct reasoning subspace.

</details>


<div id='cs.LO'></div>

# cs.LO [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [Automated Tactics for Polynomial Reasoning in Lean 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13514)
*Hao Shen,Junyu Guo,Junqi Liu,Lihong Zhi*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 在Lean 4中通过证书验证方法集成外部计算机代数系统，解决Gröbner基计算问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lean 4当前的多项式形式化基于不可计算表示，不适合高效符号计算，直接计算Gröbner基在实际应用中不切实际

Method: 提出基于证书的方法，结合外部计算机代数系统（如SageMath或SymPy）和Lean 4的形式验证，使用Lean中的可计算多项式表示来导入和验证外部生成的Gröbner基计算

Result: 开发了自动化策略，支持余式验证、Gröbner基检查、理想等式、理想或根理想成员资格等任务，实现了外部符号计算与Lean 4的集成

Conclusion: 该方法为在Lean 4中集成外部符号计算提供了实用途径，同时保持了形式证明的可靠性

Abstract: Applying Gröbner basis theory to concrete problems in Lean 4 remains difficult since the current formalization of multivariate polynomials is based on a non-computable representation and is therefore not suitable for efficient symbolic computation. As a result, computing Gröbner bases directly inside Lean is impractical for realistic examples. To address this issue, we propose a certificate-based approach that combines external computer algebra systems, such as SageMath or SymPy, with formal verification in Lean 4. Our approach uses a computable representation of multivariate polynomials in Lean to import and verify externally generated Gröbner basis computations. The external solver carries out the main algebraic computations, while the returned results are verified inside Lean. Based on this method, we develop automated tactics that transfer polynomial data between Lean and the external system and certify the returned results. These tactics support tasks such as remainder verification, Gröbner basis checking, ideal equality, and ideal or radical membership. This work provides a practical way to integrate external symbolic computation into Lean 4 while preserving the reliability of formal proof.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [121] [Optimizing Control-Friendly Trajectories with Self-Supervised Residual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.02738)
*Kexin Guo,Zihan Yang,Yuhang Liu,Jindou Jia,Xiang Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出自监督残差学习和轨迹优化框架，通过混合动力学模型学习未知动态效应，优化轨迹以最小化残差物理，实现四旋翼的敏捷飞行精确跟踪


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实物理系统只能以有限精度进行解析建模，复杂机器人系统存在残余物理效应，导致跟踪激进轨迹时精度不足

Method: 1) 学习闭环模型中的未知动态效应作为名义动力学的残差，形成混合模型；2) 使用解析梯度仅需轨迹级数据进行学习，支持任意积分步长的准确长时域预测；3) 开发轨迹优化器，最小化沿轨迹的残差物理，生成对后续控制友好的轨迹

Result: 通过混合动力学模型，优化器能够生成可精确跟踪的激进运动，在四旋翼敏捷飞行中验证了方法的有效性

Conclusion: 提出的自监督残差学习和轨迹优化框架能够有效处理复杂机器人系统中的残余物理效应，实现激进轨迹的精确跟踪

Abstract: Real-world physics can only be analytically modeled with a certain level of precision for modern intricate robotic systems. As a result, tracking aggressive trajectories accurately could be challenging due to the existence of residual physics during controller synthesis. This paper presents a self-supervised residual learning and trajectory optimization framework to address the aforementioned challenges. At first, unknown dynamic effects on the closed-loop model are learned and treated as residuals of the nominal dynamics, jointly forming a hybrid model. We show that learning with analytic gradients can be achieved using only trajectory-level data while enjoying accurate long-horizon prediction with an arbitrary integration step size. Subsequently, a trajectory optimizer is developed to compute the optimal reference trajectory with the residual physics along it minimized. It ends up with trajectories that are friendly to the following control level. The agile flight of quadrotors illustrates that by utilizing the hybrid dynamics, the proposed optimizer outputs aggressive motions that can be precisely tracked.

</details>


### [122] [Olfactory pursuit: catching a moving odor source in complex flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13121)
*Maurizio Carbone,Lorenzo Piro,Robin A. Heinonen,Luca Biferale,Massimo Cencini,Antonio Celani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种用于气味追踪的混合策略，结合信息增益和贪婪值函数，在目标运动持续性变化时实现近最优性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气味追踪面临信号间歇性、湍流混合和延迟等挑战，传统信息论策略在目标持续运动时表现不佳，需要开发更有效的搜索策略

Method: 将嗅觉追踪建模为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程，使用离散跑-转模型计算准最优策略，并引入结合Infotaxis信息增益和完全可观测控制问题贪婪值函数的混合策略

Result: 混合策略在所有持续性时间下实现近最优性能，显著优于纯探索方法，在复杂搜索场景中表现出强鲁棒性，包括连续跑-转运动、模型失配和更精确的羽流动力学

Conclusion: 目标运动的预测推断是有效嗅觉追踪的关键要素，该研究为信息贫乏、动态演化环境中的搜索提供了通用框架

Abstract: Locating and intercepting a moving target from possibly delayed, intermittent sensory signals is a paradigmatic problem in decision-making under uncertainty, and a fundamental challenge for, e.g., animals seeking prey or mates and autonomous robotic systems. Odor signals are intermittent, strongly mixed by turbulent-like transport, and typically lag behind the true target position, thereby complicating localization. Here, we formulate olfactory pursuit as a partially observable Markov decision process in which an agent maintains a joint belief over the target's position and velocity. Using a discrete run-and-tumble model, we compute quasi-optimal policies by numerically solving the Bellman equation and benchmark them against well-established information-theoretic strategies such as Infotaxis. We show that purely exploratory policies are near-optimal when the target frequently reorients, but fail dramatically when the target exhibits persistent motion. We thus introduce a computationally efficient hybrid policy that combines the information-gain drive of Infotaxis with a "greedy" value function derived from an associated fully observable control problem. Our heuristic achieves near-optimal performance across all persistence times and substantially outperforms purely exploratory approaches. Moreover, our proposal demonstrates strong robustness even in more complex search scenarios, including continuous run-and-tumble prey motion with moderate persistence time, model mismatch, and more accurate plume dynamics representation. Our results identify predictive inference of target motion as the key ingredient for effective olfactory pursuit and provide a general framework for search in information-poor, dynamically evolving environments.

</details>


### [123] [Unified Meta-Representation and Feedback Calibration for General Disturbance Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.02762)
*Zihan Yang,Jindou Jia,Meng Wang,Yuhang Liu,Kexin Guo,Xiang Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于元学习和反馈校准在线适应的通用扰动估计框架，有效处理非结构性时变扰动


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器人应用中，未知时变扰动的精确控制是一个开放性问题。现有基于元学习的方法需要共享环境结构表示，缺乏对现实非结构性扰动的灵活性，且表示误差和分布偏移会导致预测精度严重下降。

Method: 构建基于元学习和反馈校准在线适应的通用扰动估计框架：1）从有限时间窗口的过去观测中提取特征，学习无需预定义结构假设的统一表示；2）通过状态反馈机制校准在线适应过程，衰减由表示和泛化性限制引起的学习残差。

Result: 理论分析表明在线学习误差和扰动估计误差可同时收敛。通过统一元表示，该框架能有效估计多种快速变化扰动，四旋翼飞行实验验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一种通用扰动估计框架，通过元学习和反馈校准在线适应，克服了现有方法对结构性假设的依赖，实现了对非结构性时变扰动的有效估计。

Abstract: Precise control in modern robotic applications is always an open issue due to unknown time-varying disturbances. Existing meta-learning-based approaches require a shared representation of environmental structures, which lack flexibility for realistic non-structural disturbances. Besides, representation error and the distribution shifts can lead to heavy degradation in prediction accuracy. This work presents a generalizable disturbance estimation framework that builds on meta-learning and feedback-calibrated online adaptation. By extracting features from a finite time window of past observations, a unified representation that effectively captures general non-structural disturbances can be learned without predefined structural assumptions. The online adaptation process is subsequently calibrated by a state-feedback mechanism to attenuate the learning residual originating from the representation and generalizability limitations. Theoretical analysis shows that simultaneous convergence of both the online learning error and the disturbance estimation error can be achieved. Through the unified meta-representation, our framework effectively estimates multiple rapidly changing disturbances, as demonstrated by quadrotor flight experiments. See the project page for video, supplementary material and code: https://nonstructural-metalearn.github.io.

</details>


### [124] [Multi-modal panoramic 3D outdoor datasets for place categorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13142)
*Hojung Jung,Yuki Oto,Oscar M. Mozos,Yumi Iwashita,Ryo Kurazume*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了两个多模态全景3D室外数据集（MPO），用于语义场所分类，包含森林、海岸、住宅区、城市区和室内/外停车场六类。第一个数据集包含650个静态全景扫描，第二个数据集包含34,200个实时全景扫描，均在福冈市采集并公开可用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为语义场所分类任务提供多模态全景3D室外数据集，解决现有数据集中缺乏高质量、大规模全景3D点云数据的问题，支持室外环境感知研究。

Method: 使用两种激光扫描仪采集数据：FARO激光扫描仪获取密集静态全景扫描（900万点），Velodyne激光扫描仪获取稀疏实时全景扫描（7万点）。数据集包含六类语义场所，并比较了多种分类方法。

Result: 在两个数据集上实现了高分类准确率：密集数据集达到96.42%，稀疏数据集达到89.67%。数据集已公开可用，为室外语义场所分类研究提供了基准。

Conclusion: 提出的MPO数据集为多模态全景3D室外语义场所分类提供了有价值的资源，展示了在不同密度点云数据上的有效分类性能，推动了室外环境感知技术的发展。

Abstract: We present two multi-modal panoramic 3D outdoor (MPO) datasets for semantic place categorization with six categories: forest, coast, residential area, urban area and indoor/outdoor parking lot. The first dataset consists of 650 static panoramic scans of dense (9,000,000 points) 3D color and reflectance point clouds obtained using a FARO laser scanner with synchronized color images. The second dataset consists of 34,200 real-time panoramic scans of sparse (70,000 points) 3D reflectance point clouds obtained using a Velodyne laser scanner while driving a car. The datasets were obtained in the city of Fukuoka, Japan and are publicly available in [1], [2]. In addition, we compare several approaches for semantic place categorization with best results of 96.42% (dense) and 89.67% (sparse).

</details>


### [125] [Weakly-supervised Learning for Physics-informed Neural Motion Planning via Sparse Roadmap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13204)
*Ruiqi Ni,Yuchen Liu,Ahmed H. Qureshi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出H-NTFields框架，结合稀疏路图的弱监督与物理信息PDE正则化，解决复杂多房间环境中现有物理信息方法难以扩展的问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于物理信息的方法（如NTFields）在复杂多房间环境中难以扩展，单纯增加采样数量无法解决局部极小值或保证全局一致性

Method: 提出分层神经时间场（H-NTFields），结合稀疏路图的弱监督（提供旅行时间的上下界作为全局拓扑锚点）与物理信息PDE正则化（强制局部几何保真度和障碍物感知传播）

Result: 在18个Gibson环境和真实机器人平台上，H-NTFields显著提高了鲁棒性，同时通过连续值表示实现快速摊销推理

Conclusion: H-NTFields通过弱监督与物理信息正则化的结合，有效解决了复杂环境中运动规划的可扩展性问题

Abstract: The motion planning problem requires finding a collision-free path between start and goal configurations in high-dimensional, cluttered spaces. Recent learning-based methods offer promising solutions, with self-supervised physics-informed approaches such as Neural Time Fields (NTFields) solving the Eikonal equation to learn value functions without expert demonstrations. However, existing physics-informed methods struggle to scale in complex, multi-room environments, where simply increasing the number of samples cannot resolve local minima or guarantee global consistency. We propose Hierarchical Neural Time Fields (H-NTFields), a weakly-supervised framework that combines weak supervision from sparse roadmaps with physics-informed PDE regularization. The roadmap provides global topological anchors through upper and lower bounds on travel times, while PDE losses enforce local geometric fidelity and obstacle-aware propagation. Experiments on 18 Gibson environments and real robotic platforms show that H-NTFields substantially improves robustness over prior physics-informed methods, while enabling fast amortized inference through a continuous value representation.

</details>


### [126] [Capability-Aware Heterogeneous Control Barrier Functions for Decentralized Multi-Robot Safe Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13245)
*Joonkyung Kim,Yanze Zhang,Wenhao Luo,Yiwei Lyu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CA-HCBF框架，用于异构机器人团队的去中心化安全导航，通过能力感知的责任分配确保安全约束的物理可实现性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去中心化方法通常假设机器人同质性，导致异构机器人之间安全要求解释不一致，可能产生物理上无法实现的避障义务，从而引发安全违规或死锁

Method: 1) 推导规范二阶控制仿射表示，统一完整和非完整机器人在加速度级控制；2) 提出基于支撑函数的方向能力度量，量化机器人执行运动意图的能力；3) 设计成对责任分配机制，按运动能力比例分配安全负担；4) 引入可行性感知裁剪机制，将分配约束在物理可实现范围内

Result: 在最多30个异构机器人的仿真和物理多机器人演示中，相比基线方法显示出改进的安全性和任务效率，验证了在不同运动学约束机器人间的实际适用性

Conclusion: CA-HCBF框架为异构机器人团队提供了一致的去中心化安全执行和能力感知协调，解决了异构动态系统中相对阶不匹配和不可行约束分配的问题

Abstract: Safe navigation for multi-robot systems requires enforcing safety without sacrificing task efficiency under decentralized decision-making. Existing decentralized methods often assume robot homogeneity, making shared safety requirements non-uniformly interpreted across heterogeneous agents with structurally different dynamics, which could lead to avoidance obligations not physically realizable for some robots and thus cause safety violations or deadlock. In this paper, we propose Capability-Aware Heterogeneous Control Barrier Function (CA-HCBF), a decentralized framework for consistent safety enforcement and capability-aware coordination in heterogeneous robot teams. We derive a canonical second-order control-affine representation that unifies holonomic and nonholonomic robots under acceleration-level control via canonical transformation and backstepping, preserving forward invariance of the safe set while avoiding relative-degree mismatch across heterogeneous dynamics. We further introduce a support-function-based directional capability metric that quantifies each robot's ability to follow its motion intent, deriving a pairwise responsibility allocation that distributes the safety burden proportionally to each robot's motion capability. A feasibility-aware clipping mechanism further constrains the allocation to each agent's physically achievable range, mitigating infeasible constraint assignments common in dense decentralized CBF settings. Simulations with up to 30 heterogeneous robots and a physical multi-robot demonstration show improved safety and task efficiency over baselines, validating real-world applicability across robots with distinct kinematic constraints.

</details>


### [127] [GeoVision-Enabled Digital Twin for Hybrid Autonomous-Teleoperated Medical Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13248)
*Parham Kebria,Soheil Sabri,Laura J Brattain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于数字孪生的混合自主-遥操作医疗响应系统架构，通过实时同步的平台状态、环境动态和患者状况虚拟表示，增强远程临床和操作人员的态势感知与决策能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对灾害影响和基础设施有限环境中的紧急医疗响应需求，传统地面控制界面在远程医疗响应中存在局限性，需要更直观、实时更新的系统状态和环境表示来支持远程临床决策。

Method: 提出数字孪生架构，集成感知和自适应导航功能，创建与物理系统实时同步的虚拟表示，包括系统状态、环境动态、患者状况和任务目标，为远程用户提供直观的操作界面。

Result: 数字孪生架构相比传统地面控制界面，能够为远程临床和操作人员提供持续更新的虚拟表示，增强态势感知能力，支持更明智的决策制定。

Conclusion: 基于GeoVision能力的数字孪生架构为混合自主-遥操作医疗响应系统提供了创新的解决方案，通过实时同步的虚拟表示显著改善了远程医疗响应中的操作效率和临床决策质量。

Abstract: Remote medical response systems are increasingly being deployed to support emergency care in disaster-affected and infrastructure-limited environments. Enabled by GeoVision capabilities, this paper presents a Digital Twin architecture for hybrid autonomous-teleoperated medical response systems. The proposed framework integrates perception and adaptive navigation with a Digital Twin, synchronized in real-time, that mirrors system states, environmental dynamics, patient conditions, and mission objectives. Unlike traditional ground control interfaces, the Digital Twin provides remote clinical and operational users with an intuitive, continuously updated virtual representation of the platform and its operational context, enabling enhanced situational awareness and informed decision-making.

</details>


### [128] [Boundary Sampling to Learn Predictive Safety Filters via Pontryagin's Maximum Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13325)
*James Dallas,Thomas Lew,John Talbot,Jonathan DeCastro,Somil Bansal,John Subosits*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 使用庞特里亚金极大值原理采样边界轨迹，指导学习型汉密尔顿-雅可比可达性分析的数据收集，提高安全滤波器学习效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的安全滤波器在高维系统中面临数据收集效率问题，需要包含可能导致约束违反的状态信息，但在复杂高维系统中难以高效采样

Method: 使用庞特里亚金极大值原理(PMP)表征勉强避免安全违规的边界轨迹，用这些轨迹指导学习型汉密尔顿-雅可比可达性分析的数据收集，将学习集中在安全关键状态附近，然后将学习到的控制屏障值函数直接用于安全滤波

Result: 在共享控制自动驾驶赛车应用中，PMP采样提高了学习效率，实现了更快的收敛速度、更低的故障率、改进的安全集重构，且计算时间约为3毫秒

Conclusion: PMP引导的数据采样方法能有效提高学习型安全滤波器的效率，特别是在复杂高维系统中，通过集中学习安全关键区域，实现了快速收敛和可靠的安全保障

Abstract: Safety filters provide a practical approach for enforcing safety constraints in autonomous systems. While learning-based tools scale to high-dimensional systems, their performance depends on informative data that includes states likely to lead to constraint violation, which can be difficult to efficiently sample in complex, high-dimensional systems. In this work, we characterize trajectories that barely avoid safety violations using the Pontryagin Maximum Principle. These boundary trajectories are used to guide data collection for learned Hamilton-Jacobi Reachability, concentrating learning efforts near safety-critical states to improve efficiency. The learned Control Barrier Value Function is then used directly for safety filtering. Simulations and experimental validation on a shared-control automotive racing application demonstrate PMP sampling improves learning efficiency, yielding faster convergence, reduced failure rates, and improved safe set reconstruction, with wall times around 3ms.

</details>


### [129] [Utilizing Inpainting for Keypoint Detection for Vision-Based Control of Robotic Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13309)
*Sreejani Chatterjee,Venkatesh Mullur,Abhinav Gandhi,Berk Calli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于自然视觉特征的机器人视觉伺服框架，通过图像修复技术生成无标记训练数据，结合实时修复和UKF滤波实现鲁棒的关键点检测与控制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发能够在机器人上无法或不希望放置外部标记的场景下，使用自然视觉特征进行视觉伺服控制的方法。传统方法依赖精确的相机标定和机器人模型，限制了实际应用。

Method: 1. 数据收集：在机器人上放置ArUco标记，标记中心作为关键点标签；2. 图像修复：使用修复方法去除标记并重建被遮挡区域，生成无标记的标注图像；3. 训练关键点检测算法；4. 运行时引入第二个修复模型实时重建机器人被遮挡区域；5. 集成无迹卡尔曼滤波器(UKF)优化关键点估计

Result: 成功实现了基于自然机器人特征的模型无关、纯视觉控制策略，在完全可见和部分遮挡条件下均获得良好的控制效果

Conclusion: 该方法通过创新的图像修复技术消除了对相机标定和机器人模型的依赖，实现了鲁棒的自然特征视觉伺服控制，为无标记机器人视觉控制提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: In this paper we present a novel visual servoing framework to control a robotic manipulator in the configuration space by using purely natural visual features. Our goal is to develop methods that can robustly detect and track natural features or keypoints on robotic manipulators that would be used for vision-based control, especially for scenarios where placing external markers on the robot is not feasible or preferred at runtime. For the model training process of our data driven approach, we create a data collection pipeline where we attach ArUco markers along the robot's body, label their centers as keypoints, and then utilize an inpainting method to remove the markers and reconstruct the occluded regions. By doing so, we generate natural (markerless) robot images that are automatically labeled with the marker locations. These images are used to train a keypoint detection algorithm, which is used to control the robot configuration using natural features of the robot. Unlike the prior methods that rely on accurate camera calibration and robot models for labeling training images, our approach eliminates these dependencies through inpainting. To achieve robust keypoint detection even in the presence of occlusion, we introduce a second inpainting model, this time to utilize during runtime, that reconstructs occluded regions of the robot in real time, enabling continuous keypoint detection. To further enhance the consistency and robustness of keypoint predictions, we integrate an Unscented Kalman Filter (UKF) that refines the keypoint estimates over time, adding to stable and reliable control performance. We obtained successful control results with this model-free and purely vision-based control strategy, utilizing natural robot features in the runtime, both under full visibility and partial occlusion.

</details>


### [130] [Vectorizing Projection in Manifold-Constrained Motion Planning for Real-Time Whole-Body Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13323)
*Shrutheesh R Iyer,I-Chia Chang,Andrew Z. Liu,Yan Gu,Zachary Kingston*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种CPU SIMD加速的流形约束运动规划器，通过并行化投影式约束满足方法，实现比现有方法快100-1000倍的速度提升，首次实现实时约束运动规划


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流形约束运动规划算法处理复杂系统（如人形机器人）时耗时数十秒，无法满足动态环境中的实时应用需求

Method: 采用CPU SIMD并行加速技术，重新审视基于投影的约束满足方法，将相关组件转换为可并行化结构，利用SIMD并行性规划满足约束的解决方案

Result: 相比现有最先进方法实现100-1000倍的速度提升，首次实现实时约束运动规划，并在真实人形机器人上展示了实时全身准静态规划生成

Conclusion: 通过SIMD并行化方法显著加速流形约束运动规划，使复杂系统的实时约束运动规划成为可能，为人形机器人等动态环境应用提供了实用解决方案

Abstract: Many robot planning tasks require satisfaction of one or more constraints throughout the entire trajectory. For geometric constraints, manifold-constrained motion planning algorithms are capable of planning collision-free path between start and goal configurations on the constraint submanifolds specified by task. Current state-of-the-art methods can take tens of seconds to solve these tasks for complex systems such as humanoid robots, making real-world use impractical, especially in dynamic settings. Inspired by recent advances in hardware accelerated motion planning, we present a CPU SIMD-accelerated manifold-constrained motion planner that revisits projection-based constraint satisfaction through the lens of parallelization. By transforming relevant components into parallelizable structures, we use SIMD parallelism to plan constraint satisfying solutions. Our approach achieves up to 100-1000x speed-ups over the state-of-the-art, making real-time constrained motion planning feasible for the first time. We demonstrate our planner on a real humanoid robot and show real-time whole-body quasi-static plan generation. Our work is available at https://commalab.org/papers/mcvamp/.

</details>


### [131] [Singularity Avoidance in Inverse Kinematics: A Unified Treatment of Classical and Learning-based Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13405)
*Vishnu Rudrasamudram,Hariharasudan Malaichamee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文系统综述了机器人逆运动学中的奇异性问题，提出了统一分类框架，并通过实验评估了12种IK求解器在Franka Panda机器人上的性能，发现纯学习方法表现不佳，而混合方法通过经典优化改进能显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇异性配置会导致任务空间移动性丧失、关节速度无界和求解器发散，现有综述未能将经典奇异性鲁棒IK方法与快速发展的基于学习的方法联系起来，需要提供一个统一的处理框架。

Method: 提出了一个系统分类法，按保留的几何结构和鲁棒性保证（形式化与经验性）对方法进行分类。建立了统一的处理框架，涵盖雅可比正则化、黎曼可操作性跟踪、约束优化和现代数据驱动范式。提出了基准测试协议，在Franka Panda机器人上评估了12种IK求解器，通过四个互补面板测量误差退化、速度放大、分布外鲁棒性和计算成本。

Result: 纯学习方法即使在条件良好的目标上也失败（MLP：0%成功率，约10毫米平均误差），而混合预热启动架构（IKFlow：59%到100%，CycleIK：0%到98.6%，GGIK：0%到100%）通过经典细化拯救了学习求解器，DLS能从高达207毫米的初始误差收敛。DLS在奇异性附近表现最佳，而学习方法和混合方法在良好条件下表现更好。

Conclusion: 该论文填补了经典奇异性鲁棒IK与学习型方法之间的综述空白，提供了统一的分类框架和基准测试协议。实验表明混合方法结合了学习和经典优化的优势，而纯学习方法在奇异性处理上仍有局限。需要进一步深入研究奇异性区域的评估。

Abstract: Singular configurations cause loss of task-space mobility, unbounded joint velocities, and solver divergence in inverse kinematics (IK) for serial manipulators. No existing survey bridges classical singularity-robust IK with rapidly growing learning-based approaches. We provide a unified treatment spanning Jacobian regularization, Riemannian manipulability tracking, constrained optimization, and modern data-driven paradigms. A systematic taxonomy classifies methods by retained geometric structure and robustness guarantees (formal vs. empirical). We address a critical evaluation gap by proposing a benchmarking protocol and presenting experimental results: 12 IK solvers are evaluated on the Franka Panda under position-only IK across four complementary panels measuring error degradation by condition number, velocity amplification, out-of-distribution robustness, and computational cost. Results show that pure learning methods fail even on well-conditioned targets (MLP: 0% success, approx. 10 mm mean error), while hybrid warm-start architectures - IKFlow (59% to 100%), CycleIK(0% to 98.6%), GGIK (0% to 100%) - rescue learned solvers via classical refinement, with DLS converging from initial errors up to 207 mm. Deeper singularity-regime evaluation is identified as immediate future work.

</details>


### [132] [Empirical Prediction of Pedestrian Comfort in Mobile Robot Pedestrian Encounters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13677)
*Alireza Jafari,Hong-Son Nguyen,Yen-Chen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究通过实验分析移动机器人与行人交互的运动学变量与行人主观舒适度的关系，开发了基于最小距离、最小投影碰撞时间和综合变量的舒适度预测器，为社交合规机器人路径规划提供量化依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前移动机器人在公共空间（如人行道）运行时，大多关注客观安全性（如避碰算法），但缺乏对行人主观舒适度的考虑。量化舒适度是机器人理解并响应人类情感的主要挑战，阻碍了社交合规机器人的发展。

Method: 进行一对一的实验试验，每个试验涉及一个移动机器人和一名志愿者。通过统计分析行人报告的舒适度与运动学变量（如最小距离、最小投影碰撞时间等）的关系，基于经验发现设计三种舒适度估计器/预测器：基于最小距离、基于最小投影碰撞时间、以及综合所有研究变量的复合估计器。

Result: 运动学变量与行人主观舒适度之间存在中度但显著的相关性。复合预测器达到最高的预测率和分类性能，其优势比为3.67，意味着当它识别行人为舒适时，该行人实际舒适而非不舒适的可能性几乎是4倍。

Conclusion: 该研究为将行人感受纳入路径规划提供了舒适度量化工具，有助于开发更社交合规的机器人。复合预测器在识别行人舒适度方面表现出最佳性能，为未来机器人行为设计提供了实证基础。

Abstract: Mobile robots joining public spaces like sidewalks must care for pedestrian comfort. Many studies consider pedestrians' objective safety, for example, by developing collision avoidance algorithms, but not enough studies take the pedestrian's subjective safety or comfort into consideration. Quantifying comfort is a major challenge that hinders mobile robots from understanding and responding to human emotions. We empirically look into the relationship between the mobile robot-pedestrian interaction kinematics and subjective comfort. We perform one-on-one experimental trials, each involving a mobile robot and a volunteer. Statistical analysis of pedestrians' reported comfort versus the kinematic variables shows moderate but significant correlations for most variables. Based on these empirical findings, we design three comfort estimators/predictors derived from the minimum distance, the minimum projected time-to-collision, and a composite estimator. The composite estimator employs all studied kinematic variables and reaches the highest prediction rate and classifying performance among the predictors. The composite predictor has an odds ratio of 3.67. In simple terms, when it identifies a pedestrian as comfortable, it is almost 4 times more likely that the pedestrian is comfortable rather than uncomfortable. The study provides a comfort quantifier for incorporating pedestrian feelings into path planners for more socially compliant robots.

</details>


### [133] [Robust Energy-Aware Routing for Air-Ground Cooperative Multi-UAV Delivery in Wind-Uncertain Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13441)
*Tianshun Li,Hongliang Lu,Yanggang Sheng,Zhongzhen Wang,Haoang Li,Xinhu Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BER是一个在线风险敏感规划框架，用于风敏感卡车辅助无人机配送，通过时间依赖能量图路由，在动态风环境中平衡即时能耗和不确定性感知风险，显著提高任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现实的卡车-无人机物流系统中，无人机必须在部分可观测的时变风条件下安全配送包裹并返回。现有路由方法大多假设静态或确定性能量模型，在动态风环境中不可靠，因此需要确保风不确定性下的能量可行性。

Method: 提出电池高效路由(BER)框架，将问题建模为时间依赖能量图上的路由问题，边成本根据风致空气动力学效应演化。BER持续评估返回可行性，平衡瞬时能耗和不确定性感知风险，并嵌入分层空地配送架构，结合任务分配、路由和分散轨迹执行。

Result: 在Unreal Engine环境中生成的合成ER图和准真实风日志上的广泛仿真表明，BER相比静态和贪婪基线显著提高了任务成功率，减少了风致故障。

Conclusion: 结果强调了集成实时能量预算和环境感知对于动态风条件下无人机配送规划的重要性，BER框架为风敏感卡车辅助无人机配送提供了有效的在线风险敏感规划解决方案。

Abstract: Ensuring energy feasibility under wind uncertainty is critical for the safety and reliability of UAV delivery missions. In realistic truck-drone logistics systems, UAVs must deliver parcels and safely return under time-varying wind conditions that are only partially observable during flight. However, most existing routing approaches assume static or deterministic energy models, making them unreliable in dynamic wind environments. We propose Battery-Efficient Routing (BER), an online risk-sensitive planning framework for wind-sensitive truck-assisted UAV delivery. The problem is formulated as routing on a time dependent energy graph whose edge costs evolve according to wind-induced aerodynamic effects. BER continuously evaluates return feasibility while balancing instantaneous energy expenditure and uncertainty-aware risk. The approach is embedded in a hierarchical aerial-ground delivery architecture that combines task allocation, routing, and decentralized trajectory execution. Extensive simulations on synthetic ER graphs generated in Unreal Engine environments and quasi-real wind logs demonstrate that BER significantly improves mission success rates and reduces wind-induced failures compared with static and greedy baselines. These results highlight the importance of integrating real-time energy budgeting and environmental awareness for UAV delivery planning under dynamic wind conditions.

</details>


### [134] [RobotPan: A 360$^\circ$ Surround-View Robotic Vision System for Embodied Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13476)
*Jiahao Ma,Qiang Zhang,Peiran Liu,Zeran Su,Pihai Sun,Gang Han,Wen Zhao,Wei Cui,Zhang Zhang,Zhiyuan Xu,Renjing Xu,Jian Tang,Miaomiao Liu,Yijie Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RobotPan系统，结合六摄像头与LiDAR实现360°全景感知，通过前馈框架从稀疏视图预测度量尺度紧凑3D高斯，支持实时渲染、重建与流式传输，适用于机器人导航与操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人视觉接口存在视野局限（仅前向）或多摄像头切换繁琐的问题，且运动抖动会导致头戴显示器中的模拟器晕动症。需要一种能提供完整360°视觉覆盖并满足实时部署约束的系统。

Method: 提出RobotPan前馈框架：1）使用六摄像头+LiDAR多传感器系统；2）将多视图特征提升到统一球坐标系表示；3）使用分层球形体素先验解码高斯，近机器人处分配精细分辨率，远处分配粗糙分辨率以减少计算冗余；4）在线融合支持长序列，动态更新内容同时防止静态区域无限增长。

Result: 实验表明RobotPan在质量上与现有前馈重建和视图合成方法相当，同时产生更少的高斯，实现实用的实时部署。发布了专门用于360°新视图合成和度量3D重建的多传感器数据集。

Conclusion: RobotPan系统解决了机器人全景感知的关键挑战，通过紧凑的3D高斯表示和分层球坐标处理，在保证质量的同时实现实时性能，为机器人导航、操作和远程控制提供了有效的视觉接口解决方案。

Abstract: Surround-view perception is increasingly important for robotic navigation and loco-manipulation, especially in human-in-the-loop settings such as teleoperation, data collection, and emergency takeover. However, current robotic visual interfaces are often limited to narrow forward-facing views, or, when multiple on-board cameras are available, require cumbersome manual switching that interrupts the operator's workflow. Both configurations suffer from motion-induced jitter that causes simulator sickness in head-mounted displays. We introduce a surround-view robotic vision system that combines six cameras with LiDAR to provide full 360$^\circ$ visual coverage, while meeting the geometric and real-time constraints of embodied deployment. We further present \textsc{RobotPan}, a feed-forward framework that predicts \emph{metric-scaled} and \emph{compact} 3D Gaussians from calibrated sparse-view inputs for real-time rendering, reconstruction, and streaming. \textsc{RobotPan} lifts multi-view features into a unified spherical coordinate representation and decodes Gaussians using hierarchical spherical voxel priors, allocating fine resolution near the robot and coarser resolution at larger radii to reduce computational redundancy without sacrificing fidelity. To support long sequences, our online fusion updates dynamic content while preventing unbounded growth in static regions by selectively updating appearance. Finally, we release a multi-sensor dataset tailored to 360$^\circ$ novel view synthesis and metric 3D reconstruction for robotics, covering navigation, manipulation, and locomotion on real platforms. Experiments show that \textsc{RobotPan} achieves competitive quality against prior feed-forward reconstruction and view-synthesis methods while producing substantially fewer Gaussians, enabling practical real-time embodied deployment. Project website: https://robotpan.github.io/

</details>


### [135] [Beyond Conservative Automated Driving in Multi-Agent Scenarios via Coupled Model Predictive Control and Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13891)
*Saeed Rahmani,Gözde Körpe,Zhenlin,Xu,Bruno Brito,Simeon Craig Calvert,Bart van Arem*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MPC-RL集成框架，用于无信号交叉口的多智能体导航，结合MPC的结构化约束处理与RL的适应性学习，在安全性和效率之间取得更好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无信号交叉口的自动驾驶面临复杂多车交互挑战，需要平衡安全与效率。传统MPC依赖人工规则导致过于保守，而端到端RL缺乏安全保障且泛化能力差，需要结合两者优势。

Method: 提出集成MPC-RL框架，将MPC的结构化优化约束与RL的适应性学习相结合。MPC提供安全约束基础，RL学习优化策略以改善保守行为，实现安全与效率的平衡。

Result: MPC-RL在三种交通密度下均优于单独MPC和端到端RL：碰撞率降低21%，成功率提高6.5%（相比纯MPC）。零样本迁移到高速公路合流场景时，MPC方法显著优于PPO，显示MPC骨架的跨场景鲁棒性。训练中损失稳定更快，学习负担减轻。

Conclusion: 集成MPC-RL框架能改善多智能体交叉口场景中安全性能与效率的平衡，MPC组件为跨驾驶环境的泛化提供了坚实基础，代码已开源。

Abstract: Automated driving at unsignalized intersections is challenging due to complex multi-vehicle interactions and the need to balance safety and efficiency. Model Predictive Control (MPC) offers structured constraint handling through optimization but relies on hand-crafted rules that often produce overly conservative behavior. Deep Reinforcement Learning (RL) learns adaptive behaviors from experience but often struggles with safety assurance and generalization to unseen environments. In this study, we present an integrated MPC-RL framework to improve navigation performance in multi-agent scenarios. Experiments show that MPC-RL outperforms standalone MPC and end-to-end RL across three traffic-density levels. Collectively, MPC-RL reduces the collision rate by 21% and improves the success rate by 6.5% compared to pure MPC. We further evaluate zero-shot transfer to a highway merging scenario without retraining. Both MPC-based methods transfer substantially better than end-to-end PPO, which highlights the role of the MPC backbone in cross-scenario robustness. The framework also shows faster loss stabilization than end-to-end RL during training, which indicates a reduced learning burden. These results suggest that the integrated approach can improve the balance between safety performance and efficiency in multi-agent intersection scenarios, while the MPC component provides a strong foundation for generalization across driving environments. The implementation code is available open-source.

</details>


### [136] [RadarSplat-RIO: Indoor Radar-Inertial Odometry with Gaussian Splatting-Based Radar Bundle Adjustment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13492)
*Pou-Chun Kung,Yuan Tian,Zhengqin Li,Yue Liu,Eric Whitmire,Wolf Kienzle,Hrvoje Benko*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 首个基于高斯溅射的雷达束调整框架，利用全距离-方位-多普勒数据联合优化雷达位姿与场景几何，显著降低漂移误差


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 雷达SLAM相比视觉和激光雷达在恶劣天气和光照条件下更具鲁棒性，但现有雷达SLAM主要依赖帧间里程计，导致显著漂移。虽然回环检测可以纠正长期误差，但需要重访地点且依赖鲁棒的地点识别。视觉里程计通常使用束调整联合优化位姿和地图，但雷达的等效束调整方法尚未充分探索。

Method: 提出首个基于高斯溅射的雷达束调整框架，使用密集可微的场景表示方法。该方法利用全距离-方位-多普勒数据联合优化雷达传感器位姿和场景几何，首次将多帧束调整的优势引入雷达SLAM。与现有雷达惯性里程计前端集成，共同工作。

Result: 在多个室内场景中，雷达束调整相比先前的雷达惯性里程计取得显著提升：平均绝对平移误差减少90%，平均绝对旋转误差减少80%。显著降低了位姿漂移并提高了鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 这是首个将束调整引入雷达SLAM的工作，通过高斯溅射表示实现了雷达位姿与场景几何的联合优化，大幅降低了传统雷达SLAM的漂移问题，为雷达SLAM提供了新的优化框架。

Abstract: Radar is more resilient to adverse weather and lighting conditions than visual and Lidar simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM). However, most radar SLAM pipelines still rely heavily on frame-to-frame odometry, which leads to substantial drift. While loop closure can correct long-term errors, it requires revisiting places and relies on robust place recognition. In contrast, visual odometry methods typically leverage bundle adjustment (BA) to jointly optimize poses and map within a local window. However, an equivalent BA formulation for radar has remained largely unexplored. We present the first radar BA framework enabled by Gaussian Splatting (GS), a dense and differentiable scene representation. Our method jointly optimizes radar sensor poses and scene geometry using full range-azimuth-Doppler data, bringing the benefits of multi-frame BA to radar for the first time. When integrated with an existing radar-inertial odometry frontend, our approach significantly reduces pose drift and improves robustness. Across multiple indoor scenes, our radar BA achieves substantial gains over the prior radar-inertial odometry, reducing average absolute translational and rotational errors by 90% and 80%, respectively.

</details>


### [137] [A transformable slender microrobot inspired by nematode parasites for interventional endovascular surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13513)
*Xin Yang,Dongliang Fan,Yunteng Ma,Yuxuan Liao,Diancheng Li,U Kei Cheang,Bo Peng,Hongqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 受线虫启发开发的可变形细长磁性微机器人，具有高灵活性、运动能力和通过复杂血管的能力，可用于介入手术、栓塞治疗和药物输送。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心血管疾病每年导致约1790万人死亡，治疗面临血管空间狭小、拓扑结构复杂和手术风险高的挑战。现有机器人难以兼顾多功能性和机动性，因此需要开发新型微机器人来解决这些问题。

Method: 受在人体血管系统中生活的线虫启发，开发可变形细长磁性微机器人。通过实验和分析优化制造工艺和几何结构，最终制造出长径比大于100的细长原型（直径小于200微米，长度大于20毫米），在超薄聚合物绳上均匀分布磁性珠子，头部有一个大珠子。

Result: 原型展示出卓越的灵活性（最大曲率0.904 mm⁻¹）和运动能力（最大速度125 mm/s）。机器人能够通过半径0.84 mm的急转弯和三维空间分布的孔洞。在介入手术应用中，成功导航通过狭窄血管模具，包裹并运输比自身重95倍的药物，并在目标位置释放。在栓塞应用中，能够变形缠绕在动脉瘤模型中，并通过直径1.2 mm的医用针头成功抽吸和注射。

Conclusion: 线虫启发的可变形细长磁性微机器人在灵活性、运动能力和通过复杂血管结构方面表现出色，展示了在介入手术、药物输送和栓塞治疗中的潜在应用价值，为解决心血管疾病治疗中的挑战提供了新思路。

Abstract: Cardiovascular diseases account for around 17.9 million deaths per year globally, the treatment of which is challenging considering the confined space and complex topology of the vascular network and high risks during operations. Robots, although promising, still face the dilemma of possessing versatility or maneuverability after decades of development. Inspired by nematodes, the parasites living, feeding, and moving in the human body's vascular system, this work develops a transformable slender magnetic microrobot. Based on the experiments and analyses, we optimize the fabrication and geometry of the robot and finally create a slender prototype with an aspect ratio larger than 100 (smaller than 200 microns in diameter and longer than 20 mm in length), which possesses uniformly distributed magnetic beads on the body of an ultrathin polymer string and a big bead on the head. This prototype shows great flexibility (largest curvature 0.904 mm-1) and locomotion capability (the maximum speed: 125 mm/s). Moreover, the nematode-inspired robot can pass through sharp turns with a radius of 0.84 mm and holes distributed in three-dimensional (3D) space. We also display the potential application in interventional surgery of the microrobot by navigating it through a narrow blood vessel mold to wrap and transport a drug (95 times heavier than the robot) by deforming the robot's slender body and releasing the drug to the aim position finally. Moreover, the robot also demonstrates the possible applications in embolization by transforming and winding itself into an aneurysms phantom and exhibits its outstanding injectability by being successfully withdrawn and injected through a medical needle (diameter: 1.2 mm) of a syringe.

</details>


### [138] [Stability Principle Underlying Passive Dynamic Walking of Rimless Wheel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13530)
*Fumihiko Asano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 重新分析无轮缘轮被动行走的稳定性原理，通过线性化运动方程研究稳定性与能量守恒定律的关系


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然已知无轮缘轮在重力作用下的被动步态总是渐近稳定且周期为1，但对其内在稳定性原理的研究仍有深入空间。本文旨在通过数学分析更好地理解这一固有稳定性原理

Method: 基于支撑相运动方程的线性化分析，研究稳定性与能量守恒定律之间的关系，通过数学分析揭示内在稳定性原理

Result: 通过数学分析提供了对无轮缘轮被动行走固有稳定性原理的更深入理解，阐明了稳定性与能量守恒定律之间的内在联系

Conclusion: 通过重新考虑支撑相稳定性并分析其与能量守恒的关系，本文深化了对无轮缘轮被动行走固有稳定性原理的理解，为这一简单模型的稳定性机制提供了更完整的理论解释

Abstract: Rimless wheels are known as the simplest model for passive dynamic walking. It is known that the passive gait generated only by gravity effect always becomes asymptotically stable and 1-period because a rimless wheel automatically achieves the two necessary conditions for guaranteeing the asymptotic stability; one is the constraint on impact posture and the other is the constraint on restored mechanical energy. The asymptotic stability is then easily shown by the recurrence formula of kinetic energy. There is room, however, for further research into the inherent stability principle. In this paper, we reconsider the stability of the stance phase based on the linearization of the equation of motion, and investigate the relation between the stability and energy conservation law. Through the mathematical analysis, we provide a greater understanding of the inherent stability principle.

</details>


### [139] [Skill-informed Data-driven Haptic Nudges for High-dimensional Human Motor Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12583)
*Ankur Kamboj,Rajiv Ranganathan,Xiaobo Tan,Vaibhav Srivastava*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于数据驱动的触觉推动反馈优化框架，利用学习者技能估计解决高维冗余运动空间中新运动任务的学习挑战


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在高维冗余运动空间中学习新运动任务的挑战，传统方法难以有效指导学习者，需要根据学习者技能状态提供个性化反馈

Method: 1) 使用输入-输出隐马尔可夫模型(IOHMM)建模触觉推动下的人类运动学习随机动态，解耦潜在技能演化与可观测表现；2) 基于预测模型将触觉推动反馈设计问题表述为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程(POMDP)；3) 推导最小化长期表现成本的最优推动策略

Result: 30名参与者研究显示：POMDP策略组相比启发式反馈组和无反馈组，运动效率和终点精度显著提升；协同分析表明POMDP组更快发现高效的低维运动表征

Conclusion: 提出的数据驱动框架能有效设计个性化触觉推动反馈，加速高维运动任务学习，促进学习者发现高效运动策略

Abstract: In this work, we propose a data-driven framework to design optimal haptic nudge feedback leveraging the learner's estimated skill to address the challenge of learning a novel motor task in a high-dimensional, redundant motor space. A nudge is a series of vibrotactile feedback delivered to the learner to encourage motor movements that aid in task completion. We first model the stochastic dynamics of human motor learning under haptic nudges using an Input-Output Hidden Markov Model (IOHMM), which explicitly decouples latent skill evolution from observable performance measures. Leveraging this predictive model, we formulate the haptic nudge feedback design problem as a Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (POMDP). This allows us to derive an optimal nudging policy that minimizes long-term performance cost and implicitly guides the learner toward superior skill states. We validate our approach through a human participant study (N=30) involving a high-dimensional motor task rendered through a hand exoskeleton. Results demonstrate that participants trained with the POMDP-derived policy exhibit significantly accelerated movement efficiency and endpoint accuracy compared to groups receiving heuristic-based feedback or no feedback. Furthermore, synergy analysis reveals that the POMDP group discovers efficient low-dimensional motor representations more rapidly.

</details>


### [140] [Evolvable Embodied Agent for Robotic Manipulation via Long Short-Term Reflection and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13533)
*Jianzong Wang,Botao Zhao,Yayun He,Junqing Peng,Xulong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EEAgent框架利用视觉语言模型实现机器人自我进化，通过长短时反思优化机制动态调整提示，在复杂任务中实现最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人需要能够基于环境和反馈进行自适应和进化。传统方法存在训练需求大、跨任务泛化困难、缺乏可解释性等限制。提示学习为无需大量训练即可自我进化的机器人提供了新机会，但从任务成功和失败中提取有意义的洞察仍然具有挑战性。

Method: 提出可进化具身智能体（EEAgent）框架，利用大型视觉语言模型（VLMs）进行环境解释和策略规划。为了增强对过往经验的反思，提出了长短时反思优化（LSTRO）机制，该机制基于过往经验和新学到的教训动态优化提示，促进持续自我进化。

Result: 在六个VIMA-Bench任务上的评估表明，该方法达到了新的最先进水平，在复杂场景中显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: EEAgent框架通过结合视觉语言模型和长短时反思优化机制，实现了机器人的持续自我进化，提高了任务成功率，为解决通用机器人面临的挑战提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Achieving general-purpose robotics requires empowering robots to adapt and evolve based on their environment and feedback. Traditional methods face limitations such as extensive training requirements, difficulties in cross-task generalization, and lack of interpretability. Prompt learning offers new opportunities for self-evolving robots without extensive training, but simply reflecting on past experiences.However, extracting meaningful insights from task successes and failures remains a challenge. To this end, we propose the evolvable embodied agent (EEAgent) framework, which leverages large vision-language models (VLMs) for better environmental interpretation and policy planning. To enhance reflection on past experiences, we propose a long short-term reflective optimization (LSTRO) mechanism that dynamically refines prompts based on both past experiences and newly learned lessons, facilitating continuous self-evolution, thereby enhancing overall task success rates. Evaluations on six VIMA-Bench tasks reveal that our approach sets a new state-of-the-art, notably outperforming baselines in complex scenarios.

</details>


### [141] [A Mechanistic Analysis of Sim-and-Real Co-Training in Generative Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13645)
*Yu Lei,Minghuan Liu,Abhiram Maddukuri,Zhenyu Jiang,Yuke Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文通过理论分析和实证研究揭示了sim-and-real协同训练的内在机制，识别出结构化表示对齐和重要性重加权两个关键效应，并提出了改进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管协同训练（结合有限真实数据和丰富替代数据）在机器人策略训练中广泛应用且经验上成功，但其有效性的内在机制和条件仍不清楚。论文旨在深入理解协同训练的工作原理，特别是sim-and-real协同训练的内在机制。

Method: 采用理论分析和实证研究相结合的方法：1）通过理论分析识别协同训练的内在效应；2）在玩具模型上进行控制实验验证；3）在sim-and-sim和sim-and-real机器人操作任务上进行广泛实验验证；4）基于分析提出改进方法。

Result: 识别出协同训练的两个关键内在效应：1）结构化表示对齐效应（主要作用），反映跨域表示对齐与域可辨别性之间的平衡；2）重要性重加权效应（次要作用），源于动作权重的域依赖性调制。验证了这些效应，并基于分析提出了优于先前方法的简单改进方法。

Conclusion: 该研究为协同训练机制提供了统一的理论解释，揭示了结构化表示对齐和重要性重加权两个内在效应，提出的改进方法优于现有技术，有助于推动协同训练方向的研究。

Abstract: Co-training, which combines limited in-domain real-world data with abundant surrogate data such as simulation or cross-embodiment robot data, is widely used for training generative robot policies. Despite its empirical success, the mechanisms that determine when and why co-training is effective remain poorly understood. We investigate the mechanism of sim-and-real co-training through theoretical analysis and empirical study, and identify two intrinsic effects governing performance. The first, \textbf{``structured representation alignment"}, reflects a balance between cross-domain representation alignment and domain discernibility, and plays a primary role in downstream performance. The second, the \textbf{``importance reweighting effect"}, arises from domain-dependent modulation of action weighting and operates at a secondary level. We validate these effects with controlled experiments on a toy model and extensive sim-and-sim and sim-and-real robot manipulation experiments. Our analysis offers a unified interpretation of recent co-training techniques and motivates a simple method that consistently improves upon prior approaches. More broadly, our aim is to examine the inner workings of co-training and to facilitate research in this direction.

</details>


### [142] [Vision-and-Language Navigation for UAVs: Progress, Challenges, and a Research Roadmap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13654)
*Hanxuan Chen,Jie Zheng,Siqi Yang,Tianle Zeng,Siwei Feng,Songsheng Cheng,Ruilong Ren,Hanzhong Guo,Shuai Yuan,Xiangyue Wang,Kangli Wang,Ji Pei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 关于无人机视觉语言导航（UAV-VLN）领域的全面综述，涵盖任务定义、技术演进、资源生态系统、现实挑战及未来研究方向


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机视觉语言导航是具身人工智能的关键挑战，需要系统梳理该领域从任务定义到最新进展的完整脉络，建立方法论分类体系，分析现实部署障碍，并为未来研究提供指导路线图

Method: 建立方法论分类体系，追踪从早期模块化方法、深度学习方法到当前基于大型基础模型（VLMs、VLA模型）的智能体系统技术演进；系统综述仿真器、数据集、评估指标等关键资源；批判性分析仿真-现实差距、动态室外环境感知、语言歧义推理、资源受限硬件部署等核心挑战

Result: 提供了UAV-VLN领域的结构化综述，建立了从传统方法到基于生成世界模型与VLA架构融合的物理基础推理的技术演化路径；系统梳理了标准化研究所需的资源生态系统；识别了阻碍现实部署的四大关键挑战

Conclusion: 通过综合当前基准和局限性，提出了前瞻性研究路线图，指导未来探索多智能体集群协调、空地协同机器人等前沿方向，推动UAV-VLN向实际应用发展

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAV-VLN) represents a pivotal challenge in embodied artificial intelligence, focused on enabling UAVs to interpret high-level human commands and execute long-horizon tasks in complex 3D environments. This paper provides a comprehensive and structured survey of the field, from its formal task definition to the current state of the art. We establish a methodological taxonomy that charts the technological evolution from early modular and deep learning approaches to contemporary agentic systems driven by large foundation models, including Vision-Language Models (VLMs), Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, and the emerging integration of generative world models with VLA architectures for physically-grounded reasoning. The survey systematically reviews the ecosystem of essential resources simulators, datasets, and evaluation metrics that facilitates standardized research. Furthermore, we conduct a critical analysis of the primary challenges impeding real-world deployment: the simulation-to-reality gap, robust perception in dynamic outdoor settings, reasoning with linguistic ambiguity, and the efficient deployment of large models on resource-constrained hardware. By synthesizing current benchmarks and limitations, this survey concludes by proposing a forward-looking research roadmap to guide future inquiry into key frontiers such as multi-agent swarm coordination and air-ground collaborative robotics.

</details>


### [143] [Failure Identification in Imitation Learning Via Statistical and Semantic Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13788)
*Quentin Rolland,Fabrice Mayran de Chamisso,Jean-Baptiste Mouret*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FIDeL是一个独立于策略的故障检测模块，通过最优传输匹配和语义过滤来区分机器人执行中的真实故障与良性异常，在BotFails数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习策略在受控环境中表现良好，但在实际部署中面对硬件故障、意外状态等超出训练分布的情况时仍然脆弱。现有的视觉异常检测方法能检测异常状态，但无法区分故障与良性偏差。

Method: FIDeL构建正常演示的紧凑表示，通过最优传输匹配对齐输入观测以生成异常分数和热图。使用时域阈值扩展的保形预测方法，并利用视觉语言模型进行语义过滤来区分良性异常与真实故障。

Result: 在BotFails多模态机器人故障检测数据集上，FIDeL在异常检测方面比现有方法提升5.30%的AUROC，在故障检测准确率方面提升17.38%。

Conclusion: FIDeL通过结合最优传输匹配、保形预测和视觉语言模型语义过滤，有效解决了机器人模仿学习中区分真实故障与良性异常的问题，显著提升了故障检测性能。

Abstract: Imitation learning (IL) policies in robotics deliver strong performance in controlled settings but remain brittle in real-world deployments: rare events such as hardware faults, defective parts, unexpected human actions, or any state that lies outside the training distribution can lead to failed executions. Vision-based Anomaly Detection (AD) methods emerged as an appropriate solution to detect these anomalous failure states but do not distinguish failures from benign deviations. We introduce FIDeL (Failure Identification in Demonstration Learning), a policy-independent failure detection module. Leveraging recent AD methods, FIDeL builds a compact representation of nominal demonstrations and aligns incoming observations via optimal transport matching to produce anomaly scores and heatmaps. Spatio-temporal thresholds are derived with an extension of conformal prediction, and a Vision-Language Model (VLM) performs semantic filtering to discriminate benign anomalies from genuine failures. We also introduce BotFails, a multimodal dataset of real-world tasks for failure detection in robotics. FIDeL consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, yielding +5.30% percent AUROC in anomaly detection and +17.38% percent failure-detection accuracy on BotFails compared to existing methods.

</details>


### [144] [Mosaic: An Extensible Framework for Composing Rule-Based and Learned Motion Planners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13853)
*Nick Le Large,Marlon Steiner,Lingguang Wang,Willi Poh,Jan-Hendrik Pauls,Ömer Şahin Taş,Christoph Stiller*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Mosaic是一个可扩展的自动驾驶运动规划框架，通过仲裁图整合基于规则和学习型规划器，实现透明、可追溯的决策，在nuPlan基准测试中创下新记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶中安全且可解释的运动规划是一个核心挑战。基于规则的规划器具有可预测性和可解释性，但难以处理现实交通的复杂性和不确定性；学习型规划器适应性强但透明度低且存在安全隐患。需要结合两者优势的解决方案。

Method: 提出Mosaic框架，通过仲裁图实现结构化决策。将轨迹验证和评分与各规划器的轨迹生成解耦，使每个决策透明可追溯。高层轨迹验证在规划器间引入冗余，统一评分和最优轨迹选择机制结合基于规则和学习型规划器的互补优势。

Result: 在nuPlan Val14闭环基准测试中，Mosaic达到95.48 CLS-NR和93.98 CLS-R，创下新记录，同时将责任碰撞减少30%。在专注于高交互性困难场景的interPlan基准测试中，Mosaic获得54.30 CLS-R，比其最佳组成规划器提升23.3%，且无需重新训练或额外数据。

Conclusion: Mosaic框架成功整合了基于规则和学习型规划器的优势，实现了安全、可解释且高性能的自动驾驶运动规划，在多个基准测试中表现出色，为解决自动驾驶规划中的安全-性能权衡问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Safe and explainable motion planning remains a central challenge in autonomous driving. While rule-based planners offer predictable and explainable behavior, they often fail to grasp the complexity and uncertainty of real-world traffic. Conversely, learned planners exhibit strong adaptability but suffer from reduced transparency and occasional safety violations. We introduce Mosaic, an extensible framework for structured decision-making that integrates both paradigms through arbitration graphs. By decoupling trajectory verification and scoring from the generation of trajectories by individual planners, every decision becomes transparent and traceable. Trajectory verification at a higher level introduces redundancy between the planners, limiting emergency braking to the rare case where all planners fail to produce a valid trajectory. Through unified scoring and optimal trajectory selection, rule-based and learned planners with complementary strengths and weaknesses can be combined to yield the best of both worlds. In experimental evaluation on nuPlan, Mosaic achieves 95.48 CLS-NR and 93.98 CLS-R on the Val14 closed-loop benchmark, setting a new state of the art, while reducing at-fault collisions by 30% compared to either planner in isolation. On the interPlan benchmark, focused on highly interactive and difficult scenarios, Mosaic scores 54.30 CLS-R, outperforming its best constituent planner by 23.3% - all without retraining or requiring additional data. The code is available at github.com/KIT-MRT/mosaic.

</details>


### [145] [Towards Multi-Object-Tracking with Radar on a Fast Moving Vehicle: On the Potential of Processing Radar in the Frequency Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14013)
*Tim Hansen,Arturo Gomez-Chavez,Ilya Shimchik,Andreas Birk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出在频域处理雷达数据以提高对噪声和结构误差的鲁棒性，特别适用于高动态场景，并展示了基于傅里叶变换的雷达里程计方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于特征的方法对噪声和结构误差敏感，特别是在高动态场景（如车辆自运动加上未知数量的其他移动物体）中表现不佳。频域处理能提供更高的鲁棒性，并能同时获取场景中所有移动结构的信息

Method: 提出在频域处理雷达数据，使用基于相关性的方法（如配准），开发了Fourier SOFT in 2D (FS2D)方法，利用Boreas数据集进行雷达里程计实验

Result: 展示了FS2D在雷达里程计（无传感器融合）上的初步实验结果，验证了频域处理在高动态场景中的鲁棒性优势

Conclusion: 频域处理雷达数据相比传统特征方法具有显著优势：更高的噪声鲁棒性、能处理高动态场景、可同时获取所有移动结构信息，在自动驾驶（特别是赛车超车场景）中有重要应用价值

Abstract: We promote in this paper the processing of radar data in the frequency domain to achieve higher robustness against noise and structural errors, especially in comparison to feature-based methods. This holds also for high dynamics in the scene, i.e., ego-motion of the vehicle with the sensor plus the presence of an unknown number of other moving objects. In addition to the high robustness, the processing in the frequency domain has the so far neglected advantage that the underlying correlation based methods used for, e.g., registration, provide information about all moving structures in the scene. A typical automotive application case is overtaking maneuvers, which in the context of autonomous racing are used here as a motivating example. Initial experiments and results with Fourier SOFT in 2D (FS2D) are presented that use the Boreas dataset to demonstrate radar-only-odometry, i.e., radar-odometry without sensor-fusion, to support our arguments.

</details>


### [146] [Scale-Invariant Sampling in Multi-Arm Bandit Motion Planning for Object Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14026)
*Servet B. Bayraktar,Andreas Orthey,Marc Toussaint*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种尺度不变采样策略，用于解决狭窄空间物体提取问题，通过生长-收缩搜索寻找高熵采样尺度，结合PCA确定提取方向，显著提升采样效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在拆卸任务中，螺栓、螺钉、销钉等物体需要从狭窄空间移除，环境距离常为毫米级。基于采样的规划器虽能提供完备性保证，但几乎所有运动都会与环境碰撞，采样成为瓶颈

Method: 提出尺度不变采样策略：1) 使用生长-收缩搜索探索构型空间，寻找有用且高熵的采样尺度；2) 找到有用尺度后，利用主成分分析(PCA)确定物体提取的有用方向；3) 将该采样器嵌入多臂老虎机快速探索随机树(MAB-RRT)规划器中

Result: 在8个具有挑战性的3D物体提取场景(螺栓、齿轮、杆、销、插座)上测试，与经典采样策略(均匀采样、基于障碍物采样、窄通道采样)及现代策略(配对向量、基于物理规划、拆卸广度优先搜索)相比，尺度不变采样在7/8场景中将成功率提高一个数量级

Conclusion: 尺度不变采样是拆卸任务中通用物体提取的重要概念，能显著提升狭窄空间物体提取的成功率

Abstract: Object extraction tasks often occur in disassembly problems, where bolts, screws, or pins have to be removed from tight, narrow spaces. In such problems, the distance to the environment is often on the millimeter scale. Sampling-based planners can solve such problems and provide completeness guarantees. However, sampling becomes a bottleneck, since almost all motions will result in collisions with the environment. To overcome this problem, we propose a novel scale-invariant sampling strategy which explores the configuration space using a grow-shrink search to find useful, high-entropy sampling scales. Once a useful sampling scale has been found, our framework exploits this scale by using a principal components analysis (PCA) to find useful directions for object extraction. We embed this sampler into a multi-arm bandit rapidly-exploring random tree (MAB-RRT) planner and test it on eight challenging 3D object extraction scenarios, involving bolts, gears, rods, pins, and sockets.
