To evaluate our framework, we compare it with classical sampling strategies like uniform sampling, obstacle-based sampling, and narrow-passage sampling, and with modern strategies like mate vectors, physics-based planning, and disassembly breadth first search. Our experiments show that scale-invariant sampling improves success rate by one order of magnitude on 7 out of 8 scenarios. This demonstrates that scale-invariant sampling is an important concept for general purpose object extraction in disassembly tasks.

</details>


### [147] [UMI-3D: Extending Universal Manipulation Interface from Vision-Limited to 3D Spatial Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14089)
*Ziming Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UMI-3D是通用操作接口(UMI)的多模态扩展，通过集成低成本LiDAR传感器解决原系统在遮挡、动态场景和跟踪失败等问题，提升数据收集质量和策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原UMI系统依赖单目视觉SLAM，在遮挡、动态场景和跟踪失败时表现不佳，限制了其在真实世界环境中的应用。需要更鲁棒和可扩展的数据收集系统。

Method: 1) 在腕戴式接口中集成轻量级低成本LiDAR传感器，实现LiDAR中心SLAM；2) 开发硬件同步的多模态感知管道；3) 构建统一的时空校准框架，对齐视觉观测与LiDAR点云；4) 保持原有的2D视觉运动策略公式。

Result: UMI-3D显著提高了数据收集质量和可靠性，直接转化为增强的策略性能。在标准操作任务上实现高成功率，并能学习原视觉系统难以处理的任务，如大变形物体操作和关节物体操作。

Conclusion: UMI-3D通过多模态扩展解决了原UMI系统的局限性，在保持便携性和可访问性的同时，提供了更鲁棒的数据收集能力。系统硬件和软件全部开源，促进大规模数据收集和具身智能研究。

Abstract: We present UMI-3D, a multimodal extension of the Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI) for robust and scalable data collection in embodied manipulation. While UMI enables portable, wrist-mounted data acquisition, its reliance on monocular visual SLAM makes it vulnerable to occlusions, dynamic scenes, and tracking failures, limiting its applicability in real-world environments. UMI-3D addresses these limitations by introducing a lightweight and low-cost LiDAR sensor tightly integrated into the wrist-mounted interface, enabling LiDAR-centric SLAM with accurate metric-scale pose estimation under challenging conditions. We further develop a hardware-synchronized multimodal sensing pipeline and a unified spatiotemporal calibration framework that aligns visual observations with LiDAR point clouds, producing consistent 3D representations of demonstrations. Despite maintaining the original 2D visuomotor policy formulation, UMI-3D significantly improves the quality and reliability of collected data, which directly translates into enhanced policy performance. Extensive real-world experiments demonstrate that UMI-3D not only achieves high success rates on standard manipulation tasks, but also enables learning of tasks that are challenging or infeasible for the original vision-only UMI setup, including large deformable object manipulation and articulated object operation. The system supports an end-to-end pipeline for data acquisition, alignment, training, and deployment, while preserving the portability and accessibility of the original UMI. All hardware and software components are open-sourced to facilitate large-scale data collection and accelerate research in embodied intelligence: \href{https://umi-3d.github.io}{https://umi-3d.github.io}.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [148] [OVT-MLCS: An Online Visual Tool for MLCS Mining from Long or Big Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13037)
*Zhi Wang,Yanni Li,Tihua Duan,Bing Liu,Liyong Zhang,Hui Li*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出KP-MLCS算法处理大规模序列的MLCS问题，并开发可视化工具OVT-MLCS支持在线挖掘和分析


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLCS是NP难问题，现有方法无法处理长度≥1000或≥10000的大规模序列，这阻碍了各领域对大规模序列数据的开发利用

Method: 1) 提出基于关键点的KP-MLCS算法挖掘大规模序列；2) 开发紧凑表示所有MLCS并快速揭示共同模式的新方法；3) 引入实时图形可视化和序列化技术，开发在线可视化工具OVT-MLCS

Result: OVT-MLCS能够在线挖掘、存储和下载3到5000个序列规模的MLCS（以图形和文本形式），提供用户友好的交互功能，支持对挖掘结果的检查和分析

Conclusion: OVT-MLCS的功能将促进MLCS更强大和广泛的应用，解决了现有方法无法处理大规模序列的瓶颈问题

Abstract: Mining multiple longest common subsequences (\textit{MLCS}) from a set of sequences of three or more over a finite alphabet $Σ$ (a classical NP-hard problem) is an important task in a wide variety of application fields. Unfortunately, there is still no exact \textit{MLCS} algorithm/tool that can handle long (length $\ge$ 1,000) or big (length $\ge$ 10,000) sequences, which seriously hinders the development and utilization of massive long or big sequences from various application fields today. To address the challenge, we first propose a novel key point-based \textit{MLCS} algorithm for mining big sequences, called \textit{KP-MLCS}, and then present a new method, which can compactly represent all mined \textit{MLCSs} and quickly reveal common patterns among them. Furthermore, by introducing some new techniques, e.g., real-time graphic visualization and serialization, we have developed a new online visual \textit{MLCS} mining tool, called OVT-MLCS. OVT-MLCS demonstrates that it not only enables effective online mining, storing, and downloading of \textit{MLCSs} in the form of graphs and text from long or big sequences with a scale of 3 to 5000 but also provides user-friendly interactive functions to facilitate inspection and analysis of the mined \textit{MLCS}s. We believe that the functions provided by OVT-MLCS will promote stronger and wider applications of \textit{MLCS}.

</details>


### [149] [Independent subcontexts and blocks of concept lattices. Definitions and relationships to decompose fuzzy contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13039)
*Roberto G. Aragón,Jesús Medina,Eloísa Ramírez-Poussa*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 在形式概念分析中，提出了多伴随概念格框架下的独立上下文形式定义，并将其与格的分解关联，为处理不完美信息的数据集分解提供理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 处理大型数据集时，数据集分解是常用机制。在形式概念分析中，数据集被解释为上下文，独立上下文的概念在上下文分解中很重要。需要为处理不完美信息的数据集开发分解算法提供理论基础。

Method: 在多伴随概念格框架中引入独立上下文的正式定义，该定义可推广到其他模糊方法。分析了一般有界格分解为块（blocks）的方法，并将格的分解与上下文分解为独立子上下文的存在性联系起来。

Result: 建立了多伴随概念格框架下独立上下文的正式定义，证明了格的分解与上下文分解为独立子上下文之间的关联关系，为处理不完美信息的数据集分解算法开发提供了理论基础。

Conclusion: 提出的独立上下文形式定义和格分解理论为开发处理不完美信息的数据集分解算法奠定了重要基础，该框架具有可推广性，能应用于其他模糊方法。

Abstract: The decomposition of datasets is a useful mechanism in the processing of large datasets and it is required in many cases. In formal concept analysis (FCA), the dataset is interpreted as a context and the notion of independent context is relevant in the decomposition of a context. In this paper, we have introduced a formal definition of independent context within the multi-adjoint concept lattice framework, which can be translated to other fuzzy approaches. Furthermore, we have analyzed the decomposition of a general bounded lattice in pieces, that we have called blocks. This decomposition of a lattice has been related to the existence of a decomposition of a context into independent subcontexts. This study will allow to develop algorithms to decompose datasets with imperfect information.

</details>


### [150] [Decomposition of contexts into independent subcontexts based on thresholds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13040)
*Roberto G. Aragón,Jesús Medina,Eloísa Ramírez-Poussa*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出基于多伴随概念格框架中模态算子的独立子上下文检测机制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决从大型数据库中分解出较小数据集并将小数据集信息外推到原始数据库的复杂挑战，特别是在模糊形式概念分析中处理不完整和不完美数据时的知识提取复杂性

Method: 在多伴随概念格框架中使用模态算子来检测给定上下文中的独立子上下文，分析相关机制和不同性质

Result: 未在摘要中明确说明具体结果，但暗示将提出一种检测独立子上下文的机制并分析其性质

Conclusion: 该方法为解决模糊形式概念分析中数据库分解和信息外推问题提供了理论框架

Abstract: The process of decomposing databases into smaller datasets, with the objective of extrapolating the information obtained in the smaller ones to the original database, represents a relevant and complex challenge in real applications. It is particularly relevant in the context of fuzzy formal concept analysis, where the complexities of knowledge extraction from datasets characterized by incomplete and imperfect data are considerable.
  This paper will analyze a mechanism and different properties for detecting independent subcontexts from a given context, using modal operators within the multi-adjoint concept lattice framework.

</details>


### [151] [TableNet A Large-Scale Table Dataset with LLM-Powered Autonomous](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13041)
*Ruilin Zhang,Kai Yang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: TableNet：首个基于LLM的多智能体系统，通过可控参数生成表格图像并利用多样性主动学习进行表格结构识别，显著提升性能并减少训练样本需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前表格结构识别（TSR）数据集规模和质量有限，无法充分利用大语言模型的逻辑推理能力处理复杂表格布局，需要大规模高质量数据集来提升模型性能。

Method: 1. 开发首个LLM驱动的自主表格生成与识别多智能体系统；2. 生成部分：集成可控视觉、结构和语义参数合成表格图像，支持用户自定义配置；3. 识别部分：采用基于多样性的主动学习范式，从多源表格中选择性采样最有信息量的数据进行模型微调。

Result: 1. 创建TableNet数据集，支持大规模详细数据集构建；2. 在TableNet测试集上取得有竞争力的性能，同时大幅减少训练样本需求；3. 在网页爬取的真实世界表格上表现远超基于主流数据集训练的模型。

Conclusion: TableNet是首个将主动学习应用于表格结构识别的工作，能够处理行列数、合并单元格、单元格内容等多样性特征，实现了理论上无限、领域无关、样式灵活的表格图像生成，为表格相关领域研究提供了新方向。

Abstract: Table Structure Recognition (TSR) requires the logical reasoning ability of large language models (LLMs) to handle complex table layouts, but current datasets are limited in scale and quality, hindering effective use of this reasoning capacity. We thus present TableNet dataset, a new table structure recognition dataset collected and generated through multiple sources. Central to our approach is the first LLM-powered autonomous table generation and recognition multi-agent system that we developed. The generation part of our system integrates controllable visual, structural, and semantic parameters into the synthesis of table images. It facilitates the creation of a wide array of semantically coherent tables, adaptable to user-defined configurations along with annotations, thereby supporting large-scale and detailed dataset construction. This capability enables a comprehensive and nuanced table image annotation taxonomy, potentially advancing research in table-related domains. In contrast to traditional data collection methods, This approach facilitates the theoretically infinite, domain-agnostic, and style-flexible generation of table images, ensuring both efficiency and precision. The recognition part of our system is a diversity-based active learning paradigm that utilizes tables from multiple sources and selectively samples most informative data to finetune a model, achieving a competitive performance on TableNet test set while reducing training samples by a large margin compared with baselines, and a much higher performance on web-crawled real-world tables compared with models trained on predominant table datasets. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work which employs active learning into the structure recognition of tables which is diverse in numbers of rows or columns, merged cells, cell contents, etc, which fits better for diversity-based active learning.

</details>


### [152] [A Pythonic Functional Approach for Semantic Data Harmonisation in the ILIAD Project](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13042)
*Erik Johan Nystad,Francisco Martín-Recuerda*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 开发了一种Pythonic函数式方法，用于语义数据协调，简化了RDF生成过程，使数据科学家无需深入理解语义Web技术细节


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ILIAD项目中需要将异构环境数据按照海洋信息模型(OIM)进行协调，但现有方法(RML和OTTR)要求用户掌握复杂的语义Web技术细节，导致数据科学家使用困难

Method: 开发了Pythonic函数式方法，通过Python库函数调用生成正确RDF。函数按抽象层次组织：低级函数暴露OWL和RDF语法，中级函数封装本体设计模式，高级领域特定函数协调数据协调任务

Result: 根据ILIAD数据科学家的反馈，该方法满足了他们的需求，显著增强了他们参与协调活动的能力，并在ILIAD水产养殖试点中展示了适用性

Conclusion: 提出的Pythonic函数式方法成功抽象了语义Web技术复杂性，使数据科学家能够在熟悉的Python环境中高效进行语义数据协调

Abstract: Semantic data harmonisation is a central requirement in the ILIAD project, where heterogeneous environmental data must be harmonised according to the Ocean Information Model (OIM), a modular family of ontologies for enabling the implementation of interoperable Digital Twins of the Ocean. Existing approaches to Semantic Data Harmonisation, such as RML and OTTR, offer valuable abstractions but require extensive knowledge of the technical intricacies of the OIM and the Semantic Web standards, including namespaces, IRIs, OWL constructors, and ontology design patterns. Furthermore, RML and OTTR oblige practitioners to learn specialised syntaxes and dedicated tooling. Data scientists in ILIAD have found these approaches overly cumbersome and have therefore expressed the need for a solution that abstracts away these technical details while remaining seamlessly integrated into their Python-based environments. To address these requirements, we have developed a Pythonic functional approach to semantic data harmonisation that enables users to produce correct RDF through simple function calls. The functions, structured as Python libraries, encode the design patterns of the OIM and are organised across multiple levels of abstraction. Low-level functions directly expose OWL and RDF syntax, mid-level functions encapsulate ontology design patterns, and high-level domain-specific functions orchestrate data harmonisation tasks by invoking mid-level functions. According to feedback from ILIAD data scientists, this approach satisfies their requirements and substantially enhances their ability to participate in harmonisation activities. In this paper, we present the details of our Pythonic functional approach to semantic data harmonisation and demonstrate its applicability within the ILIAD Aquaculture pilot.

</details>


### [153] [Draft-Refine-Optimize: Self-Evolved Learning for Natural Language to MongoDB Query Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13045)
*Mingwei Ye,Jiaxi Zhuang,Mingjun Xu,Linfeng Zhang,Guolin Ke,Hengxing Cai*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: EvoMQL：一个自演化的自然语言到MongoDB查询语言框架，通过迭代的草稿-精炼-优化循环，结合执行反馈实现持续改进，在多个基准测试中达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言到MongoDB查询语言（NL2MQL）对于普及文档型数据库访问至关重要，但面临MQL聚合管道的过程性、深度嵌套模式和模糊值定位等独特挑战。现有方法使用静态提示或单次精炼，无法充分建模这些复杂上下文，也未能系统利用执行反馈进行持续改进。

Method: 提出EvoMQL框架，统一证据基础上下文构建与执行驱动学习，通过迭代的草稿-精炼-优化（DRO）循环。每个循环使用草稿查询触发查询感知检索，动态构建紧凑证据上下文以解决模式模糊性并将嵌套路径定位到具体值。模型通过执行奖励和课程调度进行在线策略优化，精炼后的模型反馈到后续循环实现渐进演化。

Result: EvoMQL在EAI内分布基准测试中达到76.6%的执行准确率，在TEND外分布基准测试中达到83.1%的执行准确率，分别比最强的开源基线高出9.5%和5.2%。仅使用30亿激活参数，这种闭环范式实现了NL2MQL系统在生产环境中的可扩展持续改进。

Conclusion: EvoMQL通过自演化框架成功解决了NL2MQL的独特挑战，将证据基础上下文构建与执行驱动学习相结合，通过迭代DRO循环实现持续改进，在多个基准测试中达到最先进性能，为生产环境中的NL2MQL系统提供了可扩展的持续改进范式。

Abstract: Natural Language to MongoDB Query Language (NL2MQL) is essential for democratizing access to modern document-centric databases. Unlike Text-to-SQL, NL2MQL faces unique challenges from MQL's procedural aggregation pipelines, deeply nested schemas, and ambiguous value grounding. Existing approaches use static prompting or one-shot refinement, which inadequately model these complex contexts and fail to systematically leverage execution feedback for persistent improvement. We propose EvoMQL, a self-evolved framework that unifies evidence-grounded context construction with execution-driven learning through iterative Draft-Refine-Optimize (DRO) cycles. Each cycle uses draft queries to trigger query-aware retrieval, dynamically building compact evidence contexts that resolve schema ambiguities and ground nested paths to concrete values. The model then undergoes online policy optimization with execution-based rewards and curriculum scheduling, with refined models feeding back into subsequent cycles for progressive evolution. Overall, EvoMQL achieves state-of-the-art execution accuracy of 76.6% on the EAI in-distribution benchmark and 83.1% on the TEND out-of-distribution benchmark, outperforming the strongest open-source baselines by up to 9.5% and 5.2%, respectively. With only 3B activated parameters, this closed-loop paradigm enables scalable, continuous improvement of NL2MQL systems in production.

</details>


### [154] [A Domain-Specific Language for LLM-Driven Trigger Generation in Multimodal Data Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13046)
*Philipp Reis,Philipp Rigoll,Martin Zehetner,Jacqueline Henle,Stefan Otten,Eric Sax*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出声明式框架，通过自然语言交互和领域特定语言实现意图驱动的设备端多模态数据收集，替代被动日志记录


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前数据收集系统被动且不加区分，持续记录多模态传感器流导致高存储成本并捕获无关数据，需要更智能、选择性的数据收集方法

Method: 结合自然语言交互与形式化领域特定语言(DSL)，使用大语言模型将用户需求转换为可验证、可组合的DSL程序，定义跨异构传感器的条件触发器

Result: 在车辆和机器人感知任务中，DSL方法比无约束代码生成具有更高的生成一致性和更低的执行延迟，同时保持相当的检测性能

Conclusion: 该框架用可验证的意图驱动机制替代被动日志记录，支持模块化触发器组合和在资源受限边缘平台上的并发部署，实现实时系统中的选择性多模态数据收集

Abstract: Data-driven systems depend on task-relevant data, yet data collection pipelines remain passive and indiscriminate. Continuous logging of multimodal sensor streams incurs high storage costs and captures irrelevant data. This paper proposes a declarative framework for intent-driven, on-device data collection that enables selective collection of multimodal sensor data based on high-level user requests. The framework combines natural language interaction with a formally specified domain-specific language (DSL). Large language models translate user-defined requirements into verifiable and composable DSL programs that define conditional triggers across heterogeneous sensors, including cameras, LiDAR, and system telemetry. Empirical evaluation on vehicular and robotic perception tasks shows that the DSL-based approach achieves higher generation consistency and lower execution latency than unconstrained code generation while maintaining comparable detection performance. The structured abstraction supports modular trigger composition and concurrent deployment on resource-constrained edge platforms. This approach replaces passive logging with a verifiable, intent-driven mechanism for multimodal data collection in real-time systems.

</details>


### [155] [From Natural Language to PromQL: A Catalog-Driven Framework with Dynamic Temporal Resolution for Cloud-Native Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13048)
*Twinkll Sisodia*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出基于目录的框架，将自然语言问题转换为可执行的PromQL查询，解决云原生平台中时间序列指标查询的复杂性难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代云原生平台通过Prometheus等系统暴露数千个时间序列指标，但使用PromQL等特定领域语言编写正确查询对平台工程师和站点可靠性团队仍是重大障碍，需要弥合人类意图与可观测性数据之间的鸿沟。

Method: 采用目录驱动框架，包含三个核心贡献：1) 混合指标目录，结合约2000个静态策划的基础指标和跨GPU厂商的硬件特定信号运行时发现；2) 多阶段查询管道，包括意图分类、类别感知指标路由和多维语义评分；3) 动态时间分辨率机制，解释多样自然语言时间表达式并映射到适当的PromQL持续时间语法。通过Model Context Protocol集成实现工具增强的LLM交互。

Result: 通过预计算类别索引实现亚秒级指标发现，完整管道通过目录路径约1.1秒完成。系统已部署在生产Kubernetes集群上管理AI推理工作负载，支持跨约2000个指标的自然语言查询，涵盖集群健康、GPU利用率和模型服务性能。

Conclusion: 目录驱动框架成功将自然语言问题转换为可执行的PromQL查询，显著降低了云原生平台中时间序列指标查询的复杂性，为平台工程师和SRE团队提供了更直观的可观测性访问方式。

Abstract: Modern cloud-native platforms expose thousands of time series metrics through systems like Prometheus, yet formulating correct queries in domain-specific languages such as PromQL remains a significant barrier for platform engineers and site reliability teams. We present a catalog-driven framework that translates natural language questions into executable PromQL queries, bridging the gap between human intent and observability data. Our approach introduces three contributions: (1) a hybrid metrics catalog that combines a statically curated base of approximately 2,000 metrics with runtime discovery of hardware-specific signals across GPU vendors, (2) a multi-stage query pipeline with intent classification, category-aware metric routing, and multi-dimensional semantic scoring, and (3) a dynamic temporal resolution mechanism that interprets diverse natural language time expressions and maps them to appropriate PromQL duration syntax. We integrate the framework with the Model Context Protocol (MCP) to enable tool-augmented LLM interactions across multiple providers. The catalog-driven approach achieves sub-second metric discovery through pre-computed category indices, with the full pipeline completing in approximately 1.1 seconds via the catalog path. The system has been deployed on production Kubernetes clusters managing AI inference workloads, where it supports natural language querying across approximately 2,000 metrics spanning cluster health, GPU utilization, and model-serving performance.

</details>


### [156] [Exploring Urban Land Use Patterns by Pattern Mining and Unsupervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13050)
*Zdena Dobesova,Tai Dinh,Pavel Novak*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出基于频繁项集挖掘和无监督学习的新方法，利用Copernicus Urban Atlas数据，通过土地用途共现模式识别相似城市


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市是复杂系统，受社会经济、环境和基础设施因素影响，土地用途模式是城市形态的重要方面。需要一种系统方法来识别具有相似土地用途共现模式的城市，以支持城市规划、政策制定和比较研究。

Method: 1) 数据预处理：使用Copernicus Urban Atlas数据；2) 模式挖掘：采用negFIN算法进行频繁项集挖掘；3) 后处理；4) 知识提取与可视化。生成公开可用的交易数据集，框架可扩展且源代码公开。

Result: 开发了一个完整的分析框架，包括预处理后的公开交易数据集。该方法能够识别基于土地用途共现模式的相似城市，为城市比较研究提供了系统工具。

Conclusion: 提出的方法结合频繁项集挖掘和无监督学习，为基于土地用途模式的城市相似性分析提供了有效框架。公开的数据集和源代码促进了方法的可重复性和扩展性，有助于城市规划和研究。

Abstract: Urban areas are intricate systems shaped by socioeconomic, environmental, and infrastructural factors, with land use patterns serving as aspects of urban morphology. This paper proposes a novel methodology leveraging frequent item set mining and unsupervised learning techniques to identify similar cities based on co-occurring land use patterns. The Copernicus program's Urban Atlas data are used as source data. The methodology involves data preprocessing, pattern mining using the negFIN algorithm, postprocessing, and knowledge extraction and visualization. The preprocessing of spatial datasets results in a publicly available transaction dataset. The framework is scalable and the source code is made publicly available.

</details>


### [157] [Detecting Dynamic Relationships in Object-Centric Event Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13053)
*Alessandro Gianola,Zeeshan Hameed,Marco Montali,Anjo Seidel,Mathias Weske,Sarah Winkler*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出首个形式化定义，用于在对象中心事件日志中表示和操作动态关系，确保语义明确性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对象中心流程挖掘中，对象中心事件日志对动态关系（随时间变化的关系）描述不足，导致语义模糊，需要明确的形式化定义

Method: 识别并形式化定义一组假设，这些假设允许在对象中心事件日志中以语义明确的方式表示和操作动态关系

Result: 通过评估现有日志，验证所提假设通常得到满足，确保关系语义的完全透明性

Conclusion: 首次为对象中心事件日志中的动态关系提供了形式化基础，解决了关系语义模糊问题，增强了对象中心流程挖掘的分析能力

Abstract: Object-centric process mining examines how processes interact with multiple co-evolving objects, and has gained great interest in recent years. However, object-centric event logs (OCELs) leave object relationships underspecified in several respects, especially if relationships are dynamic, i.e., they change over time. In this paper, we identify and formally define for the first time assumptions that allow to represent and manipulate dynamic relationships in OCELs in a semantically unambiguous way. We evaluate existing logs to show that our assumptions are often satisfied, ensuring full transparency of relationship semantics.

</details>
